pādagaṇhanakau lauha-vārakau bhājiyau matau||

2859.

 uddhaṃ pana tatau lauha-vārakau garubhaṇḍakaṃ|

bhiṅkārādīni sabbāni, garubhaṇḍāni haunti hi||

2860.

 bhājaitabbau ayaupattau|

tambāyauthālakāpi ca|

dhūmanaittādikaṃ  naiva|

bhājaitabbanti dīpitaṃ||

2861.

 attanā paṭiladdhaṃ taṃ, lauhabhaṇḍaṃ tu kiñ‍cipi|

na puggalikabhaugaina, bhuñ‍jitabbañhi bhikkhunā||

2862.

 kaṃsavaṭṭakalauhānaṃ, bhājanānipi sabbasau|

na puggalikabhaugaina, vaṭṭanti paribhuñ‍jituṃ||

2863.

 tipubhaṇḍaipi aisaiva, nayau ñaiyyau vibhāvinā|

na dausau saṅghikai atthi, gihīnaṃ santakaisu vā||

2864.

 khīrapāsāṇasambhūtaṃ, garukaṃ taṭṭakādikaṃ|

pādagaṇhanatau uddhaṃ, ghaṭakau garubhaṇḍakau||

2865.

 siṅgisajjhumayaṃ hāra-kūṭajaṃ phalikubbhavaṃ|

bhājanāni na vaṭṭanti, gihīnaṃ santakānipi||

2866.

 vāsi bhājaniyā khuddā, garubhaṇḍaṃ mahattarī|

tathā pharasu vaij‍jānaṃ, sirāvaidhanakampi ca||

2867.

 kuṭhāri vāsi kuddālau, garubhaṇḍaṃ nikhādanaṃ|

sikharampi ca tainaiva, gahitanti pakāsitaṃ||

2868.

 caturassamukhaṃ dauṇi-mukhaṃ vaṅkampi tattha ca|

sadaṇḍaṃ khuddakaṃ sabbaṃ, garubhaṇḍaṃ nikhādanaṃ||

2869.

 muṭṭhikamadhikaraṇī, saṇḍāsau vā tulādikaṃ|

kiñ‍ci saṅghassa din‍naṃ cai, taṃ sabbaṃ garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2870.

 nhāpitassa ca saṇḍāsau, kattarī ca mahattarī|

mahāpipphalakaṃ tun‍na-kārānaṃ garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2871.

 val‍li saṅghassa din‍nā vā, tatthajātāpi rakkhitā|

aḍḍhabāhuppamāṇāpi, garu vaittalatādikā||

2872.

 suttavākādinibbattā, raj‍jukā yauttakāni vā|

saṅghassa din‍nakālai tu, gacchanti garubhaṇḍataṃ||

2873.

 nāḷikairassa hīrai vā, vākai vā pana kainaci|

vaṭṭaitvā hi katā aika-vaṭṭāpi garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2874.

 vaiḷu  saṅghassa din‍nau vā, rakkhitau tatthajātakau|

aṭṭhaṅgulāyatau sūci-daṇḍamattau garuṃ siyā||

2875.

 chattadaṇḍasalākāyau, daṇḍau kattarayaṭṭhipi|

pādagaṇhanakā taila-nāḷī bhājaniyā imai||

2876.

 muñ‍jādīsupi yaṃ kiñ‍ci, muṭṭhimattaṃ garuṃ siyā|

tālapaṇṇādimaikampi, din‍naṃ vā tatthajātakaṃ||

2877.

 aṭṭhaṅgulappamāṇaupi, garukaṃ rittapautthakau|

mattikā pakatī vāpi, pañ‍cavaṇṇā sudhāpi vā||

2878.

 silaisādīsu vā kiñ‍ci, din‍naṃ vā tatthajātakaṃ|

tālapak‍kapamāṇaṃ tu, garubhaṇḍanti dīpitaṃ||

2879.

 val‍livaiḷādikaṃ kiñ‍ci, arakkhitamagaupitaṃ|

garubhaṇḍaṃ na hautaiva, gahaitabbaṃ yathāsukhaṃ||

2880.

 rakkhitaṃ gaupitaṃ vāpi, gahaitabbaṃ tu gaṇhatā|

samakaṃ atiraikaṃ vā, datvā phātikamaiva vā||

2881.

 añ‍janaṃ  haritālañ‍ca, tathā hiṅgu manausilā|

bhājaitabbanti viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, viñ‍ñunā vinayañ‍ñunā||

2882.

 dārubhaṇḍaipi yau kauci, sūcidaṇḍappamāṇakau|

aṭṭhaṅgulāyatau dāru-bhaṇḍakau garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2883.

 mahāaṭṭhakathāyaṃ tu, vibhajitvāva dassitaṃ|

āsandikaupi sattaṅgau, bhaddapīṭhañ‍ca pīṭhikā||

2884.

 pīṭhamaiḷakapādañ‍ca, tathāmaṇḍakavaṭṭakaṃ|

kaucchaṃ palālapīṭhañ‍ca, dhauvanai phalakampi ca||

2885.

 bhaṇḍikā muggarau caiva, vatthaghaṭṭanamuggarau|

ambaṇampi ca mañ‍jūsā, nāvā rajanadauṇikā||

2886.

 uḷuṅkaupi samuggaupi, karaṇḍampi kaṭacchupi|

aivamādi tu sabbampi, saṅghikaṃ garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2887.

 sabbaṃ dārumayaṃ gaiha-sambhāraṃ garukaṃ mataṃ|

bhājiyaṃ kappiyaṃ cammaṃ, akappiyamabhājiyaṃ||

2888.

 aiḷacammaṃ  garuṃ vuttaṃ, tathaivaudukkhalādikaṃ|

paisakārādibhaṇḍañ‍ca, kasibhaṇḍañ‍ca saṅghikaṃ||

2889.

 tathaivādhārakau patta-pidhānaṃ tālavaṇṭakaṃ|

bījanī pacchi caṅkauṭaṃ, sabbā sammuñ‍janī garu||

2890.

 yaṃ kiñ‍ci bhūmattharaṇaṃ, yau kauci kaṭasārakau|

cak‍kayuttakayānañ‍ca, sabbampi garubhaṇḍakaṃ||

2891.

 chattañ‍ca muṭṭhipaṇṇañ‍ca, visāṇaṃtumbabhājanaṃ|

upāhanāraṇīdhamma-karaṇādi lahuṃ idaṃ||

2892.

 hatthidantau visāṇañ‍ca, yathāgatamatacchitaṃ|

mañ‍capādādi yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhājanīyamaniṭṭhitaṃ||

2893.

 niṭṭhitau  tacchitau vāpi, vidhau hiṅgukaraṇḍakau|

añ‍janī ca salākāyau, bhājanī udapuñchanī||

2894.

 sabbaṃ kulālabhaṇḍampi, paribhaugārahaṃ pana|

pattaṅgārakaṭāhañ‍ca, dhūmadānaṃ kapal‍likā||

2895.

 thūpikā dīparukkhau ca, cayanacchadaniṭṭhakā|

saṅghikaṃ pana sabbampi, garubhaṇḍanti dīpitaṃ||

2896.

 pattau kañ‍canakau caiva, thālakaṃ kuṇḍikāpi ca|

ghaṭakau lauhabhaṇḍaipi, kuṇḍikāpi ca bhājiyā||

2897.

 garunā garubhaṇḍañ‍ca, thāvaraina ca thāvaraṃ|

saṅghassa parivattaitvā, gaṇhituṃ pana vaṭṭati||

2898.

 adhautaina ca pādaina, nak‍kamai sayanāsanaṃ|

al‍lapādaina vā bhikkhu, tathaiva saupāhanau||

2899.

 bhūmiyā niṭṭhubhantassa, parikammakatāya vā|

parikammakataṃ bhittiṃ, apassaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2900.

 parikammakataṃ bhūmiṃ, saṅghikaṃ mañ‍capīṭhakaṃ|

attanau santakainaiva, pattharitvāna kainaci||

2901.

 nipaj‍jitabbaṃ, sahasā, tassa niddāyatau yadi|

sarīrāvayavau kauci, mañ‍caṃ phusati duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2902.

 laumaisu  pana laumānaṃ, gaṇanāyaiva duk‍kaṭaṃ|

talaina hatthapādānaṃ, vaṭṭatak‍kamituṃ pana||

2903.

 sahassagghanakau kauci, piṇḍapātau sacīvarau|

pattau avassikaṃ bhikkhuṃ, likhitvā ṭhapitaupi ca||

2904.

 tādisau piṇḍapātauva, saṭṭhivassānamac‍cayai|

uppan‍nau saṭṭhivassassa, ṭhitikāya dadai budhau||

2905.

 uddaisabhattaṃ  bhuñ‍jitvā, jātau cai sāmaṇairakau|

gahaituṃ labhati taṃ pacchā, sāmaṇairassa pāḷiyā||

2906.

 sampuṇṇavīsavassau yau, svai uddaisaṃ labhissati|

aj‍ja sau upasampan‍nau, atītā ṭhitikā siyā||

2907.

 sacai pana salākā tu, laddhā bhattaṃ na taṃdinai|

laddhaṃ, punadinai tassa, gāhaitabbaṃ, na saṃsayau||

2908.

 uttaruttaribhaṅgassa, bhattassaikacarassa hi|

visuñhi ṭhitikā katvā, dātabbā tu salākikā||

2909.

 bhattamaiva sacai laddhaṃ, na panuttaribhaṅgakaṃ|

laddhamuttaribhaṅgaṃ vā, na laddhaṃ bhattamaiva vā||

2910.

 yaina yaina hi yaṃ yaṃ tu, na laddhaṃ, tassa tassa ca|

taṃ taṃ punadinai cāpi, gāhaitabbanti dīpitaṃ||

2911.

 saṅghuddaisādikaṃ bhattaṃ, idaṃ sattavidhampi ca|

āgantukādibhattañ‍ca, catubbidhamudīritaṃ||

2912.

 vihāravārabhattañ‍ca, nic‍cañ‍ca kuṭibhattakaṃ|

pan‍narasavidhaṃ bhattaṃ, uddiṭṭhaṃ sabbamaividha||

2913.

 pāḷimaṭṭhakathañ‍caiva, aulaukaitvā punappunaṃ|

saṅghikai pac‍cayai sammā, vibhajaiyya vicakkhaṇau||

saināsanakkhandhakakathā|

vattakkhandhakakathā

2914.

 āgantukāvāsikapiṇḍacārī-|

saināsanārañ‍ñanumaudanāsu|

vattāni bhattai gamikassa jantā-|

gharai tathā vac‍cakuṭippavaisai||

2915.

 ācariyupajjhāyakasissasaddhi-|

vihārivattānipi sabbasauva|

vattāni vuttāni catuddasaiva|

visuddhacittaina vināyakaina||

2916.

 āgantukaina ārāmaṃ, pavisantaina bhikkhunā|

chattaṃ panāpanaitabbaṃ, muñ‍citabbā upāhanā||

2917.

 auguṇṭhanaṃ na kātabbaṃ, sīsai cīvaramaiva vā|

na hi taina ca dhautabbā, pādā pānīyavārinā||

2918.

 vanditabbāva pucchitvā, vihārai vuḍḍhabhikkhunau|

kālai saināsanaṃ taina, pucchitabbañ‍ca bhikkhunā||

2919.

 vac‍caṭṭhānañ‍ca passāva-ṭṭhānaṃ pānīyamaiva ca|

paribhaujanīyaṃ saṅgha-katikaṃ gaucarādikaṃ||

2920.

 vuḍḍhamāgantukaṃ disvā, bhikkhunāvāsikainapi|

pattaṃ paṭiggahaitabbaṃ, pac‍cuggantvāna cīvaraṃ||

2921.

 āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ, tassa pādaudakampi ca|

upanikkhipitabbañ‍ca, pucchitabbañ‍ca vārinā||

2922.

 vandaiyyau pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ, tassa saināsanampi ca|

ajjhāvutthamavutthaṃ vā, gaucarāgaucarampi ca||

2923.

 vac‍caṭṭhānañ‍ca passāva-ṭṭhānaṃ saikkhakulāni ca|

pavaisai nikkhamai kālau, vattabbau pāniyādikaṃ||

2924.

 sacai sau navakau hauti|

āgatāgantukau yathā|

nisin‍nainaiva  tainassa|

sabbamāvāsibhikkhunā||

2925.

 ‘‘atra  pattaṃ ṭhapaihīti, nisīdāhīdamāsanaṃ’’|

ic‍caivaṃ pana vattabbaṃ, daiyyaṃ saināsanampi ca||

2926.

 dārumattikabhaṇḍāni, gantukāmaina bhikkhunā|

gantabbaṃ paṭisāmaitvā, thakaitvāvasathampi ca||

2927.

 āpucchitvāpi gantabbaṃ, bhikkhunā sayanāsanaṃ|

pucchitabbai asantaipi, gaupaitvā vāpi sādhukaṃ||

2928.

 sahasā pavisai nāpi, sahasā na ca nikkhamai|

nātidūrai nac‍cāsan‍nai, ṭhātabbaṃ piṇḍacārinā||

2929.

 vāmahatthaina saṅghāṭiṃ, uc‍cāraitvātha bhājanaṃ|

dakkhiṇaina paṇāmaitvā, bhikkhaṃ gaṇhaiyya paṇḍitau||

2930.

 sūpaṃ vā dātukāmāti, sal‍lakkhaiyya muhuttakaṃ|

aulaukaiyyantarā bhikkhu, na bhikkhādāyikāmukhaṃ||

2931.

 pānīyādi panānaiyyaṃ, bhikkhunārañ‍ñakainapi|

nakkhattaṃ taina yaugau ca, jānitabbā disāpi ca||

2932.

 vac‍capassāvatitthāni, bhavanti paṭipāṭiyā|

karauntassa yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2933.

 sahasā ubbhajitvā vā, na ca vac‍cakuṭiṃ visai|

uk‍kāsitvā bahi ṭhatvā, pavisai saṇikaṃ pana||

2934.

 vac‍caṃ na nitthunantaina, kātabbaṃ pana bhikkhunā|

khādatau dantakaṭṭhaṃ vā, karautau hauti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2935.

 vac‍caṃ pana na kātabbaṃ, bahiddhā vac‍cadauṇiyā|

passāvaupi na kātabbau, bahi passāvadauṇiyā||

2936.

 kharaina nāvalaikhaiyya, na kaṭṭhaṃ vac‍cakūpakai|

chaḍḍaiyya na ca pātaiyya, khaiḷaṃ passāvadauṇiyā||

2937.

 pādukāsu  ṭhitauyaiva, ubbhajaiyya vicakkhaṇau|

paṭicchādaiyya tatthaiva, ṭhatvā nikkhamanai pana||

2938.

 nācamaiyya  sacai vac‍caṃ, katvā yau salilai sati|

tassa duk‍kaṭamuddiṭṭhaṃ, muninā mauhanāsinā||

2939.

 sasaddaṃ nācamaitabbaṃ, katvā capu capūti ca|

ācamitvā sarāvaipi, saisaitabbaṃ na tūdakaṃ||

2940.

 ūhatampi adhauvitvā, nikkhamantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ|

uk‍lāpāpi sacai haunti, saudhaitabbaṃ asaisatau||

2941.

 avalaikhanakaṭṭhaina, pūrau cai pīṭharau pana|

chaḍḍaiyya kumbhi rittā cai, kumbhiṃ pūraiyya vārinā||

2942.

 anajjhiṭṭhau hi vuḍḍhaina, pātimaukkhaṃ na uddisai|

dhammaṃ na ca bhaṇai, pañhaṃ, na pucchaiyya na vissajai||

2943.

 āpucchitvā kathaintassa, vuḍḍhaṃ vuḍḍhatarāgamai|

puna āpucchanai kic‍caṃ, natthīti paridīpitaṃ||

2944.

 vuḍḍhainaikavihārasmiṃ, saddhiṃ viharatā pana|

anāpucchā hi sajjhāyau, na kātabbau kadācipi||

2945.

 uddaisaupi na kātabbau, paripucchāya kā kathā|

na ca dhammau kathaitabbau, bhikkhunā dhammacakkhunā||

2946.

 na dīpau vijjhāpaitabbau, kātabbau vā na caiva sau|

vātapānakavāṭāni, thakaiyya vivaraiyya nau||

2947.

 caṅkamai caṅkamantau ca, vuḍḍhatau parivattayai|

tampi cīvarakaṇṇaina, kāyaina na ca ghaṭṭayai||

2948.

 puratau naiva thairānaṃ, nhāyaiyya na panūpari|

uttaraṃ autarantānaṃ, dadai maggaṃ, na ghaṭṭayai||

2949.

 vattaṃ  aparipūraintau, na sīlaṃ paripūrati|

asuddhasīlau duppañ‍ñau, cittaikaggaṃ na vindati||

2950.

 vikkhittacittaunaikaggau, saddhammaṃ na ca passati|

apassamānau saddhammaṃ, dukkhā na parimuc‍cati||

2951.

 tasmā hi vattaṃ pūraiyya, jinaputtau vicakkhaṇau|

auvādaṃ buddhasaiṭṭhassa, katvā nibbānamaihiti||

vattakkhandhakakathā|

bhikkhunikkhandhakakathā

2952.

 kāyaṃ  ūruṃ thanaṃ vāpi, vivaritvāna bhikkhunī|

attanau aṅgajātaṃ vā, bhikkhussa na ca dassayai||

2953.

 bhikkhunā saha yaṃ kiñ‍ci, sampayaujaintiyāpi ca|

tatau bhāsantiyā bhikkhuṃ, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2954.

 na ca bhikkhuniyā dīghaṃ, dhāraiyya kāyabandhanaṃ|

tainaiva kāyabandhaina, thanapaṭṭaina vā pana||

2955.

 vilīvaina ca paṭṭaina, cammapaṭṭaina vā tathā|

dussapaṭṭaina vā dussa-vaiṇiyā dussavaṭṭiyā||

2956.

 na phāsukā namaitabbā, duk‍kaṭaṃ tu namaintiyā|

na ghaṃsāpaiyya samaṇī, jaghanaṃ aṭṭhikādinā||

2957.

 hatthaṃ vā hatthakaucchaṃ vā, pādaṃ vā mukhamūrukaṃ|

kauṭṭāpaiti sacai tassā, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2958.

 na mukhaṃ limpitabbaṃ tu, na cuṇṇaitabbamaiva ca|

manausilāya vāpatti, mukhaṃ lañ‍jantiyā siyā||

2959.

 aṅgarāgau  na kātabbau, mukharāgaupi vā tathā|

avaṅgaṃ na ca kātabbaṃ, na kātabbaṃ visaisakaṃ||

2960.

 aulaukanakatau rāgā, aulaukaituṃ na vaṭṭati|

ṭhātabbaṃ na ca sālaukai, sanac‍caṃ na ca kārayai||

2961.

 duk‍kaṭaṃ muninā vuttaṃ, gaṇikaṃ vuṭṭhapaintiyā|

suraṃ vā pana maṃsaṃ vā, paṇṇaṃ vā vik‍kiṇantiyā||

2962.

 vaḍḍhiṃ vāpi vaṇij‍jaṃ vā, payaujaituṃ na vaṭṭati|

tirīṭaṃ kañ‍cukaṃ vāpi, yadi dhāraiti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2963.

 dāsau vā pana dāsī vā, tathā kammakaraupi vā|

na caivupaṭṭhapaitabbau, tiracchānagataupi vā||

2964.

 na ca bhikkhuniyā sabba-nīlādiṃ pana cīvaraṃ|

dhāraitabbaṃ, na dhāraiyya, sabbaṃ namatakampi ca||

2965.

 paṭicchan‍nāpaṭicchan‍naṃ, chin‍naṃ vācchin‍namaiva vā|

purisabyañ‍janaṃ sabbaṃ, aulaukaituṃ na vaṭṭati||

2966.

 dūratauva ca passitvā, bhikkhuṃ bhikkhuniyā pana|

maggau tassa padātabbau, auk‍kamitvāna dūratau||

2967.

 bhikkhuṃ pana ca passitvā, pattaṃ bhikkhaṃ carantiyā|

nīharitvā tamuk‍kuj‍jaṃ, dassaitabbaṃ tu bhikkhunau||

2968.

 saṃvail‍likañ‍ca kātuṃ vā, dhāraituṃ kaṭisuttakaṃ|

utukālai anuñ‍ñātaṃ, utunīnaṃ mahaisinā||

2969.

 itthipausayutaṃ yānaṃ, hatthavaṭṭakamaiva vā|

pāṭaṅkī ca gilānāya, vaṭṭataivābhirūhituṃ||

2970.

 garudhammai ṭhitāyāpi, mānattaṃ tu carantiyā|

samman‍nitvā padātabbā, dutiyā pana bhikkhunī||

2971.

 yassā pabbaj‍jakālai tu, gabbhau vuṭṭhāti itthiyā|

puttau yadi ca tassāpi, dātabbā dutiyā tathā||

2972.

 mātā  labhati pāyaituṃ, bhaujaituṃ puttamattanau|

maṇḍaitumpi urai katvā, saituṃ labhati sā pana||

2973.

 ṭhapaitvā sahasaiyyaṃ tu, tasmiṃ dutiyikāya hi|

purisaisu yathāñ‍ñaisu, vattitabbaṃ tathaiva ca||

2974.

 vibbhamainaiva sā hauti, yasmā idha abhikkhunī|

tasmā bhikkhuniyā sikkhā-pac‍cakkhānaṃ na vij‍jati||

2975.

 vibbhantāya yathā tassā, puna natthūpasampadā|

gatāya titthāyatanaṃ, tathā natthūpasampadā||

2976.

 chaidanaṃ nakhakaisānaṃ, purisaihi ca vandanaṃ|

vaṇassa parikammampi, sādituṃ pana vaṭṭati||

2977.

 na vac‍cakuṭiyā vac‍cau, kātabbau yāya kāyaci|

haiṭṭhāpi vivaṭai uddhaṃ, paṭicchan‍naipi vaṭṭati||

2978.

 na ca vaṭṭati sabbattha, pal‍laṅkaina nisīdituṃ|

gilānāyaḍḍhapal‍laṅkaṃ, vaṭṭatīti pakāsitaṃ||

2979.

 na  ca bhikkhuniyārañ‍ñai, vatthabbaṃ tu kathañ‍cana|

atitthai naratitthai vā, nhāyituṃ na ca vaṭṭati||

2980.

 samaṇī gandhacuṇṇaina, yā ca vāsitamattiyā|

nhāyaiyya paṭisautai vā, tassā āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2981.

 ‘‘tvaṃyaiva paribhuñ‍jā’’ti, paribhaugatthamattanau|

din‍naṃ abhutvā añ‍ñassa, daintiyā pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

2982.

 sabbaṃ paṭiggahāpaitvā, bhikkhūhi paribhuñ‍jituṃ|

asantainupasampan‍nai, bhikkhunīnaṃ tu vaṭṭati||

bhikkhunikkhandhakakathā|

iti vinayavinicchayai khandhakakathā niṭṭhitā|

catubbidhakammakathā

2983.

 cattārimāni  kammāni, apalaukanasañ‍ñitaṃ|

ñatti ñattidutiyañ‍ca, kammaṃ ñatticatutthakaṃ||

2984.

 apalaukanakammaṃ tu, pañ‍ca ṭhānāni gacchati|

ñattikammaṃ navaṭṭhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ satta gacchati||

2985.

 tathā ñatticatutthampi, satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

nissāraṇañ‍ca ausārau, bhaṇḍukaṃ brahmadaṇḍakau||

2986.

 apalaukanakammañhi, kammalakkhaṇapañ‍camaṃ|

nissāraṇañ‍ca ausāraṃ, samaṇuddaisatau vadai||

2987.

 bhaṇḍukaṃ pabbajantaina, chan‍naina brahmadaṇḍakaṃ|

añ‍ñassapi ca kātabbau, tathārūpassa bhikkhunau||

2988.

 sabbau san‍nipatitvāna, āpucchitvāna sabbasau|

cīvarādiparikkhāraṃ, saṅghau yaṃ daiti tassa hi||

2989.

 tikkhattuṃ apalaukaitvā, bhikkhūnaṃ ruciyā pana|

aivaṃ saṅghassa dānaṃ tu, hauti taṃ kammalakkhaṇaṃ||

2990.

 nissāraṇamathausārau, upausathapavāraṇā|

sammuti caiva dānañ‍ca, paṭiggāhau ca sattamau||

2991.

 pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhanatā caiva, aṭṭhamī parikittitā|

kammassa lakkhaṇañ‍cāti, nava ṭhānāni ñattiyā||

2992.

 vinicchayai asampattai, thairassāvinayañ‍ñunau|

tassa nissāraṇā vuttā, yā sā nissāraṇāti hi||

2993.

 upasampadāpaikkhassa, āgacchausāraṇāti sā|

upausathavasaināpi, pavāraṇavasainapi||

2994.

 ñattiyā  ṭhapitattā hi, ñattikammānimai duvai|

‘‘upasampadāpaikkhañhi, anusāsaiyyaha’’nti ca||

2995.

 ‘‘itthan‍nāmamahaṃ bhikkhuṃ, pucchaiyyaṃ vinaya’’nti ca|

aivamādipavattā hi, aidisā ñatti sammuti||

2996.

 nissaṭṭhacīvarādīnaṃ, dānaṃ ‘‘dāna’’nti vuc‍cati|

āpattīnaṃ paṭiggāhau, ‘‘paṭiggāhau’’ti vuc‍cati||

2997.

 pac‍cuk‍kaḍḍhanatā nāma, pavāruk‍kaḍḍhanā matā|

‘‘imaṃ upausathaṃ katvā, kālai pavārayāmi’’ti||

2998.

 tiṇavatthārakai sabba-paṭhamā ñatti caitarā|

kammalakkhaṇamaitanti, nava ṭhānāni ñattiyā||

2999.

 ñattidutiyakammampi, satta ṭhānāni gacchati|

nissāraṇamathausāraṃ, sammutiṃ dānamaiva ca||

3000.

 uddhāraṃ daisanaṃ kamma-lakkhaṇaṃ pana sattamaṃ|

pattanik‍kuj‍janādī tu, nissārausāraṇā matā||

3001.

 sammuti nāma sīmādi, sā pañ‍cadasadhā matā|

dānaṃ kathinavatthassa, dānaṃ matakavāsasau||

3002.

 kathinassantarubbhārau, ‘‘ubbhārau’’ti pavuc‍cati|

daisanā kuṭivatthussa, vihārassa ca vatthunau||

3003.

 tiṇavatthārakammai ca, mauhāraupanatādisu|

kammavācāvasainaittha, kammalakkhaṇatā matā||

3004.

 iti  ñattidutiyassa, imai satta pakāsitā|

tathā ñatticatutthampi, satta ṭhānāni gacchati||

3005.

 nissāraṇamathausāraṃ, sammutiṃ dānaniggahaṃ|

samanubhāsanañ‍caiva, sattamaṃ kammalakkhaṇaṃ||

3006.

 sattan‍naṃ taj‍janādīnaṃ, kammānaṃ karaṇaṃ pana|

nissāraṇātha passaddhi, taisaṃ ausāraṇā matā||

3007.

 auvādau  bhikkhunīnaṃ tu, sammutīti pakāsitā|

mānattaparivāsānaṃ, dānaṃ ‘‘dāna’’nti vuc‍cati||

3008.

 puna mūlāpaṭik‍kassau, ‘‘niggahau’’ti pavuc‍cati|

ukkhittassānuvattikā, aṭṭha yāvatatīyakā||

3009.

 ariṭṭhau caṇḍakāḷī ca, aikādasa bhavantimai|

imaisaṃ tu vasā ñaiyyā, dasaikā samanubhāsanā||

3010.

 upasampadakammañ‍ca, kammamabbhānasañ‍ñitaṃ|

idaṃ ñatticatutthai tu, sattamaṃ kammalakkhaṇaṃ||

3011.

 apalaukanakammañ‍cā-palaukaitvāva kārayai|

ñattiyā dutiyaināpi, catutthaina na kārayai||

3012.

 ñattidutiyakammāni, lahukānatthi kānici|

kātabbānapalaukaitvā, sabbā sammutiyau siyuṃ||

3013.

 saisāni apalaukaitvā, kātuṃ pana na vaṭṭati|

yathāvuttanayainaiva, taina tainaiva kārayai||

catubbidhakammakathā|

kammavipattikathā

3014.

 vatthutau ñattitau caiva, anussāvanasīmatau|

parisatauti pañ‍caiva, kammadausā pakāsitā||

3015.

 sammukhākaraṇīyaṃ  yaṃ, taṃ karauti asammukhā|

kammaṃ vatthuvipan‍naṃ taṃ, adhammanti pavuc‍cati||

3016.

 asammukhākaraṇīyāni, aṭṭhaiva ca bhavanti hi|

pattanik‍kuj‍janañ‍caiva, pattassuk‍kuj‍janampi ca||

3017.

 pakāsanīyakammañ‍ca, saikkhaummattasammuti|

avandiyau tathā brahma-daṇḍau dūtūpasampadā||

3018.

 imānaṭṭha ṭhapaitvāna, saisāni pana sabbasau|

sammukhākaraṇīyāni, kammāni sugataubravi||

3019.

 ñattitau pana pañ‍caiva, vipaj‍jananayā matā|

na parāmasati vatthuñ‍ca, saṅghaṃ puggalamaiva vā||

3020.

 na parāmasati ñattiṃ vā, pacchā ñattiṃ ṭhapaiti vā|

pañ‍cahaitaihi kammāni, ñattitauva vipaj‍jarai||

3021.

 anussāvanatau pañ‍ca, kammadausā pakāsitā|

na parāmasati vatthuṃ vā, saṅghaṃ puggalamaiva vā||

3022.

 hāpaiti sāvanaṃ vāpi, sāvaitasamayaipi vā|

aivaṃ pana vipaj‍janti, anussāvanataupi ca||

3023.

 aikādasahi sīmāhi, sīmatau kammadausatā|

vuttā upausathai tāva, khandhakai sabbasau mayā||

3024.

 catuvaggaina kātabbai, kammappattā anāgatā|

chandau ca na panānītau, paṭik‍kausanti sammukhā||

3025.

 aivaṃ tivaṅgikau dausau, parisāya vasā siyā|

āgatā kammapattā ca, chandau ca na panāgatau||

3026.

 sammukhā paṭisaidhainti, dutiyai catuvaggikai|

āgatā kammapattā ca, chandaupi ca samāhaṭau||

3027.

 paṭik‍kausauva aitthatthi, tatiyai catuvaggikai|

aivaṃ pañ‍cādivaggaisu, saṅghaisu tividhaisupi||

3028.

 catutthikā  siyuṃ dausā, dasa dvai parisāvasā|

aivaṃ dvādasadhā aittha, kammāni hi vipaj‍jarai||

kammavipattikathā|

saidamaucanakathā

ka.

sauḷasaparivārassa, parivārassa sādarā|

suṇātha nipuṇai pañhai, gūḷhatthai bhaṇatau mama||

kha.

divāpaj‍jati nau rattiṃ, rattiṃyaiva ca nau divā|

kathañ‍ca paṭiggaṇhantau, na gaṇhantau kathaṃ pana||

ga.

chindantassa siyāpatti, tathaivāchindataupi ca|

chādaintassa tathāpatti-na chādaintassa bhikkhunau||

gha.

kā cāpatti samāpatti-lābhinauyaiva bhikkhunau|

asamāpattilābhissa, kā ca nāmassa sā bhavai||

ṅa.

garukaṃ bhaṇatau sac‍caṃ, alikaṃ bhaṇatau siyuṃ|

lahuṃ sac‍caṃ bhaṇantassa, musā ca bhaṇatau garuṃ||

ca.

pavisantau ca ārāmaṃ, āpaj‍jati na nikkhamaṃ|

nikkhamantauva āpatti, na caiva pavisaṃ pana|

cha.

samādiyantau asamādiyantau|

anādiyantaupi ca ādiyantau|

daintau adaintaupi siyā sadausau|

tathā karauntaupi ca nau karauntau||

ja.

āpaj‍jati ca dhāraintau, adhāraintau tathaiva ca|

dvin‍naṃ mātā pitā sāva, kathaṃ hauti? bhaṇāhi mai||

jha.

ubhataubyañ‍janā  itthī, gabbhaṃ gaṇhāti attanā|

gaṇhāpaiti paraṃ gabbhaṃ, tasmā mātāpitā ca sā||

ña.

gāmai vā yadi vārañ‍ñai, yaṃ paraisaṃ mamāyitaṃ|

na harantauva taṃ thaiyyā, kathaṃ pārājikau bhavai|

ṭa.

 

 thaiyyasaṃvāsakau aisau, liṅgasaṃvāsathainakau|

parabhaṇḍaṃ agaṇhantau, taina hauti parājitau||

ṭha.

nāriṃ rūpavatiṃ bhikkhu, rattacittau asañ‍ñatau|

maithunaṃ tāya katvāpi, na sau pārājikau kathaṃ|

ḍa.

accharāsadisaṃ  nāriṃ, supinantaina passati|

tāya maithunasaṃyaugai, kataipi na bhavissati||

ḍha.

bahiddhā gaihatau bhikkhu, itthī gabbhantaraṃ gatā|

chiddaṃ gaihassa naivatthi, kathaṃ maithunatau cutau|

ṇa.

antaudussakuṭiṭṭhaina, mātugāmaina maithunaṃ|

santhatādivasainaiva, katvā hauti parājitau||

ta.

suttai ca vinayaiyaiva, khandhakai sānulaumikai|

sabbattha nipuṇā dhīrā, imai pañhai bhaṇanti tai||

tha.

khandhakai parivārai ca, vinayai sānulaumikai|

ādarau karaṇīyauva, paṭubhāvaṃ panicchitā||

saidamaucanakathā|

pakiṇṇakavinicchayakathā

3029.

 chattaṃ paṇṇamayaṃ kiñ‍ci, bahi antau ca sabbasau|

pañ‍cavaṇṇaina suttaina, sibbituṃ na ca vaṭṭati||

3030.

 chindituṃ  aḍḍhacandaṃ vā, paṇṇai makaradantakaṃ|

ghaṭakaṃ vāḷarūpaṃ vā, laikhā daṇḍai na vaṭṭati||

3031.

 sibbituṃ aikavaṇṇaina, chattaṃ suttaina vaṭṭati|

thiratthaṃ, pañ‍cavaṇṇānaṃ, pañ‍jaraṃ vā vinandhituṃ||

3032.

 ghaṭakaṃ vāḷarūpaṃ vā, laikhā vā pana kaivalā|

bhinditvā vāpi ghaṃsitvā, dhāraituṃ pana vaṭṭati||

3033.

 ahichattakasaṇṭhānaṃ, daṇḍabundamhi vaṭṭati|

uk‍kiritvā katā laikhā, bandhanatthāya vaṭṭati||

3034.

 nānāvaṇṇaihi suttaihi, maṇḍanatthāya cīvaraṃ|

samaṃ satapadādīnaṃ, sibbituṃ na ca vaṭṭati||

3035.

 pattassa  pariyantai vā, tathā pattamukhaipi vā|

vaiṇiṃ saṅkhalikaṃ vāpi, karautau hauti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

3036.

 paṭṭampi gaṇṭhipāsānaṃ, aṭṭhakauṇādikaṃvidhiṃ|

tatthagghiyagadārūpaṃ, muggarādiṃ karaunti ca||

3037.

 tattha kak‍kaṭakakkhīni, uṭṭhāpainti na vaṭṭati|

suttā ca piḷakā tattha, duviñ‍ñaiyyāva dīpitā||

3038.

 catukauṇāva vaṭṭanti, gaṇṭhipāsakapaṭṭakā|

kaṇṇakauṇaisu suttāni, rattai chindaiyya cīvarai||

3039.

 sūcikammavikāraṃ vā, añ‍ñaṃ vā pana kiñ‍cipi|

cīvarai bhikkhunā kātuṃ, kārāpaituṃ na vaṭṭati||

3040.

 yau ca pakkhipati bhikkhu cīvaraṃ|

kañ‍jipiṭṭhakhalial‍likādisu|

vaṇṇamaṭṭhamabhipatthayaṃ paraṃ|

tassa natthi pana mutti duk‍kaṭā||

3041.

 sūcihatthamalādīnaṃ, karaṇai cīvarassa ca|

tathā kiliṭṭhakālai ca, dhauvanatthaṃ tu vaṭṭati||

3042.

 rajanai pana gandhaṃ vā, tailaṃ vā lākhamaiva vā|

kiñ‍ci pakkhipituṃ tattha, bhikkhunau na ca vaṭṭati||

3043.

 saṅkhaina maṇinā vāpi, añ‍ñainapi ca kainaci|

cīvaraṃ na ca ghaṭṭaiyya, ghaṃsitabbaṃ na dauṇiyā||

3044.

 cīvaraṃ dauṇiyaṃ katvā, nāpi ghaṭṭaiyya muṭṭhinā|

rattaṃ paharituṃ kiñ‍ci, hatthaihaiva ca vaṭṭati||

3045.

 gaṇṭhikai pana laikhā vā, piḷakā vā na vaṭṭati|

kappabinduvikārau vā, pāḷikaṇṇikabhaidatau||

3046.

 thālakassa ca pattassa, bahi antaupi vā pana|

āraggaina katā laikhā, na ca vaṭṭati kācipi||

3047.

 āraupaitvā bhamaṃ pattaṃ, maj‍jitvā cai pacanti ca|

‘‘maṇivaṇṇaṃ karissāma’’, iti kātuṃ na vaṭṭati||

3048.

 pattamaṇḍalakai  kiñ‍ci, bhittikammaṃ na vaṭṭati|

na dausau kauci tatthassa, kātuṃ makaradantakaṃ||

3049.

 na dhammakaraṇacchattai, laikhā kācipi vaṭṭati|

kucchiyaṃ vā ṭhapaitvā taṃ, laikhaṃ tu mukhavaṭṭiyaṃ||

3050.

 suttaṃ vā diguṇaṃ katvā, kauṭṭainti ca tahiṃ tahiṃ|

kāyabandhanasaubhatthaṃ, taṃ na vaṭṭati bhikkhunau||

3051.

 dasāmukhai daḷhatthāya, dvīsu antaisu vaṭṭati|

mālākammalatākamma-cittikampi na vaṭṭati||

3052.

 akkhīni tattha dassaitvā, kauṭṭitai pana kā kathā|

kak‍kaṭakkhīni vā tattha, uṭṭhāpaituṃ na vaṭṭati||

3053.

 ghaṭaṃ  daiḍḍubhasīsaṃ vā, makarassa mukhampi vā|

vikārarūpaṃ yaṃ kiñ‍ci, na vaṭṭati dasāmukhai||

3054.

 ujukaṃ macchakaṇṭaṃ vā, maṭṭhaṃ vā pana paṭṭikaṃ|

khaj‍jūripattakākāraṃ, katvā vaṭṭati kauṭṭitaṃ||

3055.

 paṭṭikā sūkarantanti, duvidhaṃ kāyabandhanaṃ|

raj‍jukā dussapaṭṭādi, sabbaṃ tassānulaumikaṃ||

3056.

 murajaṃ maddavīṇañ‍ca, daiḍḍubhañ‍ca kalābukaṃ|

raj‍juyau na ca vaṭṭanti, purimā dvaidasā siyuṃ||

3057.

 dasā pāmaṅgasaṇṭhānā, niddiṭṭhā kāyabandhanai|

aikā dviticatassau vā, vaṭṭanti na tatau paraṃ||

3058.

 aikaraj‍jumayaṃ vuttaṃ, muninā kāyabandhanaṃ|

tañ‍ca pāmaṅgasaṇṭhānaṃ, aikampi ca na vaṭṭati||

3059.

 raj‍jukai aikatau katvā, bahū aikāya raj‍juyā|

nirantarañhi vaiṭhaitvā, kataṃ vaṭṭati bandhituṃ||

3060.

 dantakaṭṭhavisāṇaṭṭhi-lauhavaiḷunaḷabbhavā|

jatusaṅkhamayāsutta-phalajā vidhakā matā||

3061.

 kāyabandhanavidhaipi, vikārau na ca vaṭṭati|

tattha tattha paricchaida-laikhāmattaṃ tu vaṭṭati||

3062.

 mālākammalatākamma-nānārūpavicittitā|

na ca vaṭṭati bhikkhūnaṃ, añ‍janī janarañ‍janī||

3063.

 tādisaṃ pana ghaṃsitvā, vaiṭhaitvā suttakaina vā|

vaḷañ‍jantassa bhikkhussa, na dausau kauci vij‍jati||

3064.

 vaṭṭā vā caturassā vā, aṭṭhaṃsā vāpi añ‍janī|

vaṭṭataivāti niddiṭṭhā, vaṇṇamaṭṭhā na vaṭṭati||

3065.

 tathāñ‍janisalākāpi, añ‍janithavikāya ca|

nānāvaṇṇaihi suttaihi, cittakammaṃ na vaṭṭati||

3066.

 aikavaṇṇaina suttaina, sipāṭiṃ yaina kainaci|

yaṃ kiñ‍ci pana sibbaitvā, vaḷañ‍jantassa vaṭṭati||

3067.

 maṇikaṃ piḷakaṃ vāpi, pipphalai ārakaṇṭakai|

ṭhapaituṃ pana yaṃ kiñ‍ci, na ca vaṭṭati bhikkhunau||

3068.

 daṇḍakaipi paricchaida-laikhāmattaṃ tu vaṭṭati|

valitvā ca nakhacchaidaṃ, karauntīti hi vaṭṭati||

3069.

 uttarāraṇiyaṃ vāpi, dhanukai pail‍ladaṇḍakai|

mālākammādi yaṃ kiñ‍ci, vaṇṇamaṭṭhaṃ na vaṭṭati||

3070.

 saṇḍāsai dantakaṭṭhānaṃ, tathā chaidanavāsiyā|

dvīsu passaisu lauhaina, bandhituṃ pana vaṭṭati||

3071.

 tathā kattaradaṇḍaipi, cittakammaṃ na vaṭṭati|

vaṭṭalaikhāva vaṭṭanti, aikā vā dvaipi haiṭṭhatau||

3072.

 visāṇai nāḷiyaṃ vāpi, tathaivāmaṇḍasārakai|

tailabhājanakai sabbaṃ, vaṇṇamaṭṭhaṃ tu vaṭṭati||

3073.

 pānīyassa uḷuṅkaipi, dauṇiyaṃ rajanassapi|

ghaṭai phalakapīṭhaipi, valayādhārakādikai||

3074.

 tathā pattapidhānai ca, tālavaṇṭai ca bījanai|

pādapuñchaniyaṃ vāpi, sammuñ‍janiyamaiva ca||

3075.

 mañ‍cai bhūmattharai pīṭhai, bhisibimbauhanaisu ca|

mālākammādikaṃ cittaṃ, sabbamaiva ca vaṭṭati||

3076.

 nānāmaṇimayatthambha-kavāṭadvārabhittikaṃ|

saināsanamanuñ‍ñātaṃ, kā kathā vaṇṇamaṭṭhakai||

3077.

 sauvaṇṇiyaṃ  dvārakavāṭabaddhaṃ|

suvaṇṇanānāmaṇibhittibhūmiṃ|

na kiñ‍ci aikampi nisaidhanīyaṃ|

saināsanaṃ vaṭṭati sabbamaiva||

3078.

 buddhaṃ dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca, na uddissa davaṃ karai|

mūgabbatādikaṃ naiva, gaṇhaiyya titthiyabbataṃ||

3079.

 kāyaṃ vā aṅgajātaṃ vā, ūruṃ vā na tu dassayai|

bhikkhunīnaṃ tu tā vāpi, na siñ‍cai udakādinā||

3080.

 vassamañ‍ñattha vuṭṭhau cai, bhāgamañ‍ñattha gaṇhati|

duk‍kaṭaṃ puna dātabbaṃ, gīvā naṭṭhaipi jaj‍jarai||

3081.

 cauditau sau sacai taihi, bhikkhūhi na dadaiyyataṃ|

dhuranikkhaipanai taisaṃ, bhaṇḍagghainaiva kārayai||

3082.

 akappiyasamādānaṃ, karautau hauti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

davā silaṃ pavijjhantau, duk‍kaṭā na ca muc‍cati||

3083.

 gihīgaupakadānasmiṃ, na dausau kauci gaṇhatau|

paricchaidanayau vuttau, saṅghacaitiyasantakai||

3084.

 yānaṃ purisasaṃyuttaṃ, hatthavaṭṭakamaiva vā|

pāṭaṅkiñ‍ca gilānassa, vaṭṭataivābhirūhituṃ||

3085.

 na ca bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ, sampayaujaiyya kiñ‍cipi|

duk‍kaṭaṃ bhikkhuniṃ rāgā, aubhāsaintassa bhikkhunau||

3086.

 bhikkhunīnaṃ havai bhikkhu, pātimaukkhaṃ na uddisai|

āpattiṃ vā sacai tāsaṃ, paṭiggaṇhaiyya duk‍kaṭaṃ||

3087.

 attanau paribhaugatthaṃ, din‍namañ‍ñassa kassaci|

paribhaugamakatvāva, dadatau pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

3088.

 asappāyaṃ  sacai sabbaṃ, apanaitumpi vaṭṭati|

aggaṃ gahaitvā dātuṃ vā, pattādīsupyayaṃ nayau||

3089.

 pañ‍cavaggūpasampadā, guṇaṅguṇaupāhanā|

cammatthārau dhuvanhānaṃ, majjhadaisai na vaṭṭati||

3090.

 sambādhassa ca sāmantā, satthakammaṃ duvaṅgulā|

vāritaṃ, vatthikammampi, sambādhaiyaiva satthunā||

3091.

 paṇṇāni aj‍jukādīnaṃ, lauṇaṃ vā uṇhayāguyā|

pakkhipitvāna pākatthaṃ, cālaituṃ na ca vaṭṭati||

3092.

 sacai parisamañ‍ñassa, upaḷālaiti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

tattha cādīnavaṃ tassa, vattuṃ pana ca vaṭṭati||

3093.

 ‘‘makkhanaṃ gūthamuttaihi, gataina nhāyituṃ viya|

kataṃ nissāya dussīlaṃ, tayā viharatā’’ti ca||

3094.

 bhattaggai yāgupānai ca, antaugāmai ca vīthiyaṃ|

andhakārai anāvaj‍jau, aikāvattau ca byāvaṭau||

3095.

 suttau khādañ‍ca bhuñ‍jantau, vac‍caṃ muttampi vā karaṃ|

vandanā tairasan‍naṃ tu, ayuttatthaina vāritā||

3096.

 naggau anupasampan‍nau, nānāsaṃvāsakaupi ca|

yau pacchā upasampan‍nau, ukkhittau mātugāmakau||

3097.

 aikādasa abhabbā ca, garukaṭṭhā ca pañ‍cimai|

vandatau duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, bāvīsati ca puggalai||

3098.

 yau purai upasampan‍nau, nānāsaṃvāsavuḍḍhakau|

dhammavādī ca sambuddhau, vandanīyā tayau imai||

3099.

 taj‍janādikatai aittha, caturau pana puggalai|

vandataupi anāpatti, taihi kammañ‍ca kubbatau||

3100.

 adhiṭṭhānaṃ  panaikassa, dvin‍naṃ vā tiṇṇamaiva vā|

diṭṭhāvikammamuddiṭṭhaṃ, tatau uddhaṃ nivāraṇaṃ||

3101.

 sandiṭṭhau hauti sambhattau, jīvatālapitaupi ca|

gahitattamanau hauti, vissāsau pañ‍cadhā siyā||

3102.

 sīladiṭṭhivipatti ca, ācārājīvasambhavā|

vipattiyau catassauva, vuttā ādic‍cabandhunā||

3103.

 tattha  appaṭikammā ca, yā ca vuṭṭhānagāminī|

āpattiyau duvai sīla-vipattīti pakāsitā||

3104.

 antaggāhikadiṭṭhi ca, yā diṭṭhi dasavatthukā|

ayaṃ diṭṭhivipattīti, duvidhā diṭṭhi dīpitā||

3105.

 daisanāgāminikā yā ca, pañ‍ca thul‍lac‍cayādikā|

vuttācāravipattīti, ācārakusalaina sā||

3106.

 kuhanādippavattau hi, micchājīvauti dīpitau|

ājīvapac‍cayāpatti, chabbidhāti pakāsitā||

3107.

 kammunā laddhisīmāhi, nānāsaṃvāsakā tayau|

ukkhittau tividhau kamma-nānāsaṃvāsakau matau||

3108.

 adhammavādipakkhasmiṃ, nisin‍nauva vicintiyaṃ|

‘‘dhammavādī panaitai’’ti, uppan‍nai pana mānasai||

3109.

 nānāsaṃvāsakau nāma, laddhiyāyaṃ pakāsitau|

tatraṭṭhau pana sau dvin‍naṃ, kammaṃ kaupaiti saṅghikaṃ||

3110.

 bahisīmāgatau sīmā-nānāsaṃvāsakau matau|

nānāsaṃvāsakā aivaṃ, tayau vuttā mahaisinā||

3111.

 cutau anupasampan‍nau, nānāsaṃvāsakā tayau|

bhikkhūnaikādasābhabbā, asaṃvāsā imai siyuṃ||

3112.

 asaṃvāsassa  sabbassa, tathā kammārahassa ca|

saṅghai ummattakādīnaṃ, paṭikkhaipau na rūhati||

3113.

 sasaṃvāsaikasīmaṭṭha-pakatattassa bhikkhunau|

vacanaina paṭikkhaipau, rūhatānantarassapi||

3114.

 bhikkhu āpaj‍jatāpattiṃ, ākāraihi panacchahi|

vuttā samaṇakappā ca, pañ‍ca, pañ‍ca visuddhiyau||

3115.

 nidānaṃ puggalaṃ vatthuṃ, vidhiṃ pañ‍ñattiyā pana|

vipattāpattanāpatti, samuṭṭhānanayampi ca||

3116.

 vaj‍jakammakriyāsañ‍ñā, cittāṇattividhiṃ pana|

tathaivaṅgavidhānañ‍ca, vaidanā kusalattikaṃ||

3117.

 sattarasavidhaṃ  aitaṃ, dassaitvā lakkhaṇaṃ budhau|

sikkhāpadaisu yaujaiyya, tattha tattha yathārahaṃ||

3118.

 nidānaṃ tattha vaisālī, tathā rājagahaṃ puraṃ|

sāvatthāḷavi kausambī, sak‍kabhaggā pakāsitā||

3119.

 dasa vaisāliyā vuttā, aikavīsaṃ giribbajai|

satāni hi cha ūnāni, tīṇi sāvatthiyā siyuṃ||

3120.

 cha panāḷaviyaṃ vuttā, aṭṭha kausambiyaṃ katā|

aṭṭha sak‍kaisu pañ‍ñattā, tayau bhaggai pakāsitā||

3121.

 taivīsatividhā vuttā, sudin‍nadhaniyādayau|

bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkhasmiṃ, ādikammikapuggalā||

3122.

 bhikkhunīnaṃ tathā pāti-maukkhasmiṃ ādikammikā|

thul‍lanandādayau satta, sabbai tiṃsa bhavanti tai||

3123.

 taruṃ  timūlaṃ navapattamainaṃ|

dvayaṅkuraṃ sattaphalaṃ chapupphaṃ|

jānāti yau dvippabhavaṃ dvisākhaṃ|

jānāti pañ‍ñattimasaisatau sau||

3124.

 iti paramamimaṃ vinicchayaṃ|

madhurapadatthamanākulaṃ tu yau|

paṭhati suṇati pucchatai ca sau|

bhavatupālisamau vinicchayai||

iti vinayavinicchayai pakiṇṇakavinicchayakathā samattā|

kammaṭṭhānabhāvanāvidhānakathā

3125.

 pāmaukkhai pātimaukkhasmiṃ, mukhai maukkhappavaisanai|

sabbadukkhakkhayai vuttai, vuttamaivitarattayaṃ||

3126.

 idaṃ  catubbidhaṃ sīlaṃ, ñatvā tattha patiṭṭhitau|

samādhiṃ puna bhāvaitvā, pañ‍ñāya parimuc‍cati||

3127.

 dasānussatiyau vuttā, kasiṇā ca dasāsubhā|

catassau appamañ‍ñāyau, tathāruppā paradvayaṃ||

3128.

 ic‍caivaṃ pana sabbampi, cattālīsavidhaṃ siyā|

kammaṭṭhānaṃ samuddiṭṭhaṃ, mammaṭṭhānaṃ manaubhunau||

3129.

 upacārappanātau ca, jhānabhaidā atik‍kamā|

vaḍḍhanāvaḍḍhanā cāpi, tathārammaṇabhūmitau||

3130.

 gahaṇā pac‍cayā bhiyyau, tathā cariyānukūlatau|

visaisau ayamaitaisu, viñ‍ñātabbau vibhāvinā||

3131.

 aṭṭhānussatiyau  sañ‍ñā-vavatthānañ‍ca tatthimai|

upacāravahā, saisā, tiṃsa jhānavahā matā||

3132.

 paṭhamajjhānikā tattha, asubhā kāyagatāsati|

ānāpānañ‍ca kasiṇā, catuk‍kajjhānikā imai||

3133.

 tikajjhānāni tissauva, appamañ‍ñātha pacchimā|

cattāraupi ca āruppā, catutthajjhānikā matā||

3134.

 atik‍kamau dvidhā vuttau, aṅgārammaṇataupi ca|

catuk‍katikajhānaisu, aṅgātik‍kamatā matā||

3135.

 catutthā appamañ‍ñāpi, aṅgātik‍kamatau siyā|

ārammaṇamatik‍kamma, āruppā pana jāyarai||

3136.

 kasiṇāni dasaivaittha, vaḍḍhaitabbāni yauginā|

saisaṃ pana ca sabbampi, na vaḍḍhaitabbamaiva taṃ||

3137.

 nimittārammaṇā tattha, kasiṇā ca dasāsubhā|

kāyai satānāpānañ‍ca, bāvīsati bhavantimai||

3138.

 saisānussatiyau aṭṭha, sañ‍ñā dhātuvavatthanaṃ|

viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naivasañ‍ñā ca, dasa dvai bhāvagaucarā||

3139.

 catassau appamañ‍ñāyau, dvai ca āruppamānasā|

imai dhammā viniddiṭṭhā, cha navattabbagaucarā||

3140.

 dasāsubhā  paṭik‍kūla-sañ‍ñā kāyagatāsati|

daivaisu na pavattanti, dvādasaivāti bhūmitau||

3141.

 tāni dvādasa bhiyyau ca, ānāpānasatīpi ca|

sabbasau tairasa vāpi, brahmalaukai na jāyarai||

3142.

 ṭhapaitvā caturāruppai, arūpāvacarai kira|

añ‍ñai pana na jāyanti, sabbai jāyanti mānusai||

3143.

 catutthaṃ kasiṇaṃ hitvā, kasiṇā ca dasāsubhā|

diṭṭhainaiva gahaitabbā, pubbabhāgai bhavanti tai||

3144.

 ānāpānañ‍ca  phuṭṭhaina, diṭṭhaina tacapañ‍cakaṃ|

mālutau diṭṭhaphuṭṭhaina, sutaina caittha saisakaṃ||

3145.

 ākāsakasiṇañ‍caittha, ṭhapaitvā kasiṇā nava|

paṭhamāruppacittassa, pac‍cayā pana jāyarai||

3146.

 bhavanti hi abhiñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kasiṇāni dasāpi ca|

tissaupi appamañ‍ñāyau, catutthassa tu pac‍cayā||

3147.

 haiṭṭhimahaiṭṭhimāruppaṃ, parassa ca parassa ca|

naivasañ‍ñā niraudhassa, pac‍cayauti pakāsitā||

3148.

 sabbai sukhavihārassa, bhavanissaraṇassa ca|

tathā bhavasukhānañ‍ca, pac‍cayāti ca dīpitā||

3149.

 asubhā dasa viñ‍ñaiyyā, tathā kāyagatāsati|

anukūlā imai rāga-caritassa visaisatau||

3150.

 catassau appamañ‍ñāyau, savaṇṇakasiṇā tathā|

anukūlā imai dausa-caritassa pakāsitā||

3151.

 vitak‍kacaritassāpi, mauhappakatinaupi ca|

ānāpānasataikāva, sappāyāti vibhāvitā||

3152.

 sañ‍ñā caiva vavatthānaṃ, maraṇūpasamai sati|

pañ‍ñāpakatinau aitai, anukūlāti dīpitā||

3153.

 ādianussaticchak‍kaṃ, saddhācaritavaṇṇitaṃ|

āruppā kasiṇā saisā, dasa sabbānurūpakā||

3154.

 aivaṃ  pabhaidatau ñatvā, kammaṭṭhānāni paṇḍitau|

cariyāyānukūlaṃ tu, taisu yaṃ attanau pana||

3155.

 taṃ gahaitvāna maidhāvī, daḷhaṃ kalyāṇamittakau|

ucchaidaṃ palibaudhānaṃ, katvā paṭhamamaiva ca||

3156.

 anurūpai vasantaina, vihārai dausavaj‍jitai|

bhāvaitvā paṭhamādīni, jhānāni pana sabbasau||

3157.

 tatau  vuṭṭhāya sappañ‍ñau, jhānamhā paṭhamāditau|

nāmarūpavavatthānaṃ, katvā kaṅkhaṃ vitīriya||

3158.

 upak‍laisai amaggauti, dasaubhāsādayau pana|

maggau vipassanāñāṇaṃ, iti jānāti paṇḍitau||

3159.

 tiṇṇaṃ taisaṃ vavatthānai, katai aittāvatā pana|

tiṇṇaṃ pana ca sac‍cānaṃ, vavatthānaṃ kataṃ siyā||

3160.

 udayabbayabhaṅgā ca, bhayādīnavanibbidā|

muñ‍citukamyatāñāṇaṃ, paṭisaṅkhānupassanā||

3161.

 saṅkhārupaikkhāñāṇañ‍ca, navamaṃ sac‍cānulaumikaṃ|

ayaṃ ‘‘paṭipadāñāṇa-dassana’’nti pakāsitā||

3162.

 tatau gautrabhucittassa, samanantaramaiva ca|

santimārammaṇaṃ katvā, jāyatai ñāṇadassanaṃ||

3163.

 ‘‘ñāṇadassanasuddhī’’ti, idaṃ ñāṇaṃ pakāsitaṃ|

pac‍cavaikkhaṇapariyantaṃ, phalaṃ tassānujāyatai||

3164.

 tainaiva ca upāyaina, bhāvaintau sau punappunaṃ|

pāpuṇāti yathā bhikkhu, saisamaggaphalāni ca||

3165.

 ic‍caivamac‍cantamavaic‍ca dhammaṃ|

viddhaṃsayitvākusalaṃ asaisaṃ|

visausayitvāna tayau bhavai sau|

upaiti santiṃ nirupādisaisaṃ||

3166.

 viñ‍ñāsak‍kamatau vāpi, pubbāparavasaina vā|

yadi akkharabandhai vā, ayuttaṃ viya dissati||

3167.

 taṃ  tathā na gahaitabbaṃ, gahaitabbamadausatau|

mayā upaparikkhitvā, katattā pana sabbasau||

3168.

 saiṭṭhassa  cauḷaraṭṭhassa, nābhibhūtai nirākulai|

sabbassa pana laukassa, gāmai sampiṇḍitai viya||

3169.

 kadalīsālatālucchu-nāḷikairavanākulai|

kamaluppalasañchan‍na-salilāsayasaubhitai||

3170.

 kāvairijalasampāta-paribhūtamahītalai|

iddhai sabbaṅgasampan‍nai, maṅgalai bhūtamaṅgalai||

3171.

 pavarākārapākāra-parikhāparivāritai|

vihārai vaiṇhudāsassa, dassanīyai manauramai||

3172.

 tīrantaruhavātira-tarurājavirājitai|

nānādijagaṇārāmai, nānārāmamanauramai||

3173.

 cārupaṅkajasaṃkiṇṇa-taḷākasamalaṅkatai|

surasaudakasampuṇṇa-varakūpaupasaubhitai||

3174.

 vicitravipulac‍cugga-varamaṇḍapamaṇḍitai|

āvāsaihi canaikaihi, ac‍cantamupasaubhitai||

3175.

 uppataina ca thūpaina, bhaitvāva dharaṇītalaṃ|

jitvāvāvahasantaina, kailāsasikharaṃ kharaṃ||

3176.

 saradambudasaṅkāsai, dassanīyai samussitai|

pasādajananai rammai, pāsādai vasatā mayā||

3177.

 vuttassa buddhasīhaina, vinayassa vinicchayau|

buddhasīhaṃ samuddissa, mama saddhivihārikaṃ||

3178.

 katauyaṃ pana bhikkhūnaṃ, hitatthāya samāsatau|

vinayassāvabaudhatthaṃ, sukhainaivāciraina ca||

3179.

 ac‍cutac‍cutavik‍kantai, kalambakulanandanai|

mahiṃ samanusāsantai, āraddhau ca samāpitau||

3180.

 yathā siddhimayaṃ pattau, antarāyaṃ vinā tathā|

sabbai sijjhantu saṅkappā, sattānaṃ dhammasaṃyutā||

3181.

 yāva  tiṭṭhati laukasmiṃ, mandārau cārukandarau|

tāva tiṭṭhatu buddhassa, sāsanaṃ kalisāsanaṃ||

3182.

 kālai sammā pavassantu, vassaṃ vassavalāhakā|

pālayantu mahīpālā, dhammatau sakalaṃ mahiṃ||

3183.

 imaṃ sārabhūtaṃ hitaṃ atthayuttaṃ|

karauntaina pattaṃ mayā yaṃ tu puñ‍ñaṃ|

ayaṃ taina laukau munindappayātaṃ|

sivaṃ vītasaukaṃ puraṃ pāpuṇātu||

iti vinayavinicchayai kammaṭṭhānabhāvanāvidhānakathā

samattā|

iti tambapaṇṇiyaina paramavaiyyākaraṇaina tipiṭakanayavidhikusalaina paramakavijanahadayapadumavanavikasanakaraina kavivaravasabhaina paramaratikaravaramadhuravacanuggāraina uragapuraina buddhadattaina racitauyaṃ vinayavinicchayau|

vinayavinicchayau samattau|

|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

uttaravinicchayau

ganthārambhakathā

1.

 sabbasattuttamaṃ  dhīraṃ, vanditvā sirasā jinaṃ|

dhammañ‍cādhammaviddhaṃsaṃ, gaṇamaṅgaṇanāsanaṃ||

2.

 yau mayā racitau sārau, vinayassa vinicchayau|

tassa dāni karissāmi, sabbānuttaramuttaraṃ||

3.

 bhaṇatau paṭhatau payuñ‍jatau|

suṇatau cintayatau panuttaraṃ|

paramaṃ abuddha buddhivaḍḍhanaṃ|

vadatau mai niratā nibaudhatha||

mahāvibhaṅgasaṅgahakathā

4.

 maithunaṃ  paṭisaivantau, kati āpattiyau phusai|

maithunaṃ paṭisaivantau, tissau āpattiyau phusai||

5.

 bhavai pārājikaṃ khaittai, yaibhuyyakkhāyitai pana|

thul‍lac‍cayaṃ mukhai vaṭṭa-katai vuttaṃ tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

6.

 adin‍naṃ ādiyantau yau|

kati āpattiyau phusai|

adin‍naṃ ādiyantau sau|

tissau āpattiyau phusai||

7.

 pañ‍camāsagghanai  vāpi, adhikai vā parājayau|

māsai vā duk‍kaṭaṃ ūnai, majjhai thul‍lac‍cayaṃ tatau||

8.

 manussajātiṃ māraintau|

kati āpattiyau phusai|

manussajātiṃ māraintau|

tissau āpattiyau phusai||

9.

 manussamuddissaupātaṃ, khaṇanai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā|

dukkhai thul‍lac‍cayaṃ jātai, matai pārājikaṃ siyā||

10.

 asantaṃ uttariṃ dhammaṃ, vadamattūpanāyikaṃ|

kati āpaj‍jatāpattī? tissau āpattiyau phusai||

11.

 asantaṃ uttariṃ dhammaṃ, bhaṇantassa parājayau|

thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pariyāyai, ñātai, nau cai tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

pārājikakathā|

12.

 bhaṇa suk‍kaṃ vimaucaintau|

kati āpattiyau phusai|

suṇa  suk‍kaṃ vimaucaintau|

tissau āpattiyau phusai||

13.

 garukaṃ yadi caitaiti, upak‍kamati muc‍cati|

dvaṅgai thul‍lac‍cayaṃ vuttaṃ, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā||

14.

 itau paṭṭhāya muñ‍citvā, pañhāpucchanamattakaṃ|

vissaj‍janavasainaiva, hauti atthavinicchayau||

15.

 itthiyā kāyasaṃsaggai, tissau āpattiyau phusai|

āmasantassa kāyaina, kāyaṃ tu garukaṃ siyā||

16.

 kāyaina kāyabaddhaṃ tu, phusaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ phusai|

paṭibaddhaina kāyaina, paṭibaddhai tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

17.

 itthiṃ  duṭṭhul‍lavācāhi, tissau aubhāsatau siyuṃ|

vaṇṇāvaṇṇaṃ vadaṃ dvin‍naṃ, maggānaṃ garukaṃ phusai||

18.

 vaṇṇādibhañ‍ñai ādissa, ubbhajāṇumadhakkhakaṃ|

hauti thul‍lac‍cayaṃ, kāya-paṭibaddhai tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

19.

 attakāmacariyāya, vadatau vaṇṇamitthiyā|

santikai garukaṃ hauti, sacai jānāti sā pana||

20.

 santikai paṇḍakassāpi, tassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā|

tiracchānagatassāpi, santikai duk‍kaṭaṃ mataṃ||

21.

 paṭiggaṇhanavīmaṃsā, pac‍cāharaṇakattikai|

sañ‍carittaṃ samāpan‍nai, garukaṃ niddisai budhau||

22.

 tassa dvaṅgasamāyaugai, hauti thul‍lac‍cayaṃ tathā|

aṅgai sati panaikasmiṃ, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

23.

 saṃyācikāya ca kuṭiṃ|

vihārañ‍ca mahal‍lakaṃ|

kārāpaiti sacai bhikkhu|

tissau āpattiyau phusai||

24.

 payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, aikapiṇḍai anāgatai|

hauti thul‍lac‍cayaṃ, tasmiṃ, piṇḍai garukamāgatai||

25.

 pārājikaina  dhammaina, bhikkhuṃ amūlakainidha|

anuddhaṃsaiti yau tassa, tissau āpattiyau siyuṃ||

26.

 aukāsaṃ na ca kāraitvā, hutvā cāvanacaitanau|

sacai caudaiti saṅghādi-saisaina saha duk‍kaṭaṃ||

27.

 aukāsaṃ pana kāraitvā, hutvā ak‍kausacaitanau|

caudaiti aumasavādai, pācittiṃ paridīpayai||

28.

 anantarasamānauva, navamai añ‍ñabhāgiyai|

sabbau āpattibhaidau hi, natthi kāci visaisatā||

29.

 saṅghassa bhaidakau bhikkhu, yāvatatiyakaṃ pana|

samanubhāsanāyaiva, gāhaṃ na paṭinissajaṃ||

30.

 ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi, kammavācāhi thul‍lataṃ|

kammavācāya ausānai, āpatti garukaṃ siyā||

31.

 bhaidānuvattakai caiva, dubbacai kuladūsakai|

saṅghabhaidakatulyauva, hauti āpattinicchayau||

saṅghādisaisakathā|

32.

 atik‍kamantau atiraikacīvaraṃ|

dasāhamāpaj‍jati aikamaiva|

nissaggipācittiyamaikarattiṃ|

ticīvaraināpi vinā vasantau||

33.

 māsaṃ atik‍kamantau hi, gahaitvā kālacīvaraṃ|

aikaṃ āpaj‍jatāpattiṃ, nissaggiyamudīritaṃ||

34.

 añ‍ñātikāya yaṃ kiñ‍ci|

purāṇacīvaraṃ pana|

dhauvāpaiti sacai tassa|

haunti āpattiyau duvai||

35.

 dhauvāpaiti payaugasmiṃ, duk‍kaṭaṃ samudāhaṭaṃ|

nissaggiyāva pācitti, hauti dhauvāpitai pana||

36.

 añ‍ñātikāya  hatthamhā, cīvaraṃ paṭigaṇhatau|

gahaṇai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti gahitai siyā||

37.

 añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ, gahapatānimaiva vā|

cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintau dvai, bhikkhu āpattiyau phusai||

38.

 viñ‍ñāpaiti  payaugasmiṃ, duk‍kaṭaṃ parikittitaṃ|

viñ‍ñāpitai ca nissaggi, pācitti pariyāputā||

39.

 bhikkhu cīvaramañ‍ñātiṃ, viñ‍ñāpaintau taduttariṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, viñ‍ñā-pitai nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

40.

 añ‍ñātakaṃ kañ‍ci upāsakaṃ vā|

upāsikaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā|

pubbaiva hutvā pana appavāritau|

vatthai vikappaṃ paṭipaj‍jamānau||

41.

 duvai āpaj‍jatāpattī, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā|

vikappaṃ pana āpan‍nai, nissaggiyamudīritaṃ||

42.

 añ‍ñātiṃ upasaṅkamma, pubbaiyaivappavāritau|

vikappaṃ cīvarai bhikkhu, āpaj‍jantau duvai phusai||

43.

 tathātiraikatikkhattuṃ, caudanāya ca bhikkhu cai|

gantvātiraikachakkhattuṃ, ṭhānainapi ca cīvaraṃ||

44.

 nipphādaiti sacai tassa, haunti āpattiyau duvai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

kathinavaggau paṭhamau|

45.

 dausā kausiyavaggassa, dvaidvaiādīsu pañ‍casu|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

46.

 gahaitvaiḷakalaumāni, tiyaujanamatik‍kamaṃ|

duk‍kaṭaṃ paṭhamai pādai, nissaggiṃ dutiyai phusai||

47.

 bhikkhu bhikkhuniyañ‍ñāya, dhauvāpaitaiḷalaumakaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, dhautai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

48.

 rūpiyaṃ  paṭigaṇhantau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, nissaggi gahitai siyā||

49.

 nānākāraṃ samāpaj‍jaṃ, saṃvauhārañ‍ca rūpiyai|

samāpan‍nai ca nissaggiṃ, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai||

50.

 nānappakārakaṃ bhikkhu, āpaj‍jai kayavik‍kayaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tasmiṃ, katai nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

kausiyavaggau dutiyau|

51.

 pattaṃ atik‍kamaintassa, dasāhamatiraikakaṃ|

tassa nissaggiyāpatti, hauti aikāva bhikkhunau||

52.

 apañ‍cabandhanai pattai, vij‍jamānaipi bhikkhunau|

añ‍ñaṃ pana navaṃ pattaṃ, caitāpaiti sacai pana||

53.

 dvai panāpattiyau bhikkhu, āpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

54.

 paṭiggahaitvā bhaisaj‍jaṃ, sattāhaṃ yau atik‍kamai|

aikaṃ nissaggiyāpattiṃ, āpaj‍jati hi sau pana||

55.

 akālai pariyaisantau, vassasāṭikacīvaraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

56.

 bhikkhunau cīvaraṃ datvā, acchindantau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, haṭai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

57.

 viñ‍ñāpaitvā sayaṃ suttaṃ, tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ|

vāyāpaiti sacai bhikkhu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai||

58.

 yau panañ‍ñātakassaiva, tantavāyai samaic‍ca cai|

vikappaṃ cīvarai bhikkhu, āpaj‍jaṃ appavāritau||

59.

 dvai  panāpattiyau sau hi, āpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

60.

 paṭiggahaitvā ac‍caika-sañ‍ñitaṃ pana cīvaraṃ|

kālaṃ atik‍kamaintau taṃ, aikaṃ nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

61.

 tiṇṇamañ‍ñataraṃ  vatthaṃ, nidahitvā gharaidhikaṃ|

chārattatau vinā taina, vasaṃ nissaggiyaṃ phusai||

62.

 jānaṃ pariṇataṃ lābhaṃ, saṅghikaṃ attanau pana|

pariṇāmaiti cai bhikkhu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai||

63.

 payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, nissaggi pariṇāmitai|

sabbattha appanāvāra-parihāni katā mayā||

pattavaggau tatiyau|

tiṃsanissaggiyakathā|

64.

 vadantassa musāvādaṃ, pañ‍ca āpattiyau siyuṃ|

manussuttaridhammai tu, abhūtasmiṃ parājayau||

65.

 caudanāya garuṃ bhikkhuṃ, amūlantimavatthunā|

pariyāyavacanai ñātai, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ||

66.

 nau cai paṭivijānāti, duk‍kaṭaṃ samudāhaṭaṃ|

sampajānamusāvādai, pācitti paridīpitā||

67.

 āpattiyau duvai vuttā, bhikkhussaumasatau pana|

pācitti upasampan‍naṃ, duk‍kaṭaṃ itaraṃ siyā||

68.

 paisuñ‍ñaharaṇai dvaipi, haunti, pācittiyaṃ pana|

upasampan‍napaisuñ‍ñai, saisai āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

69.

 padasaunupasampan‍naṃ, dhammaṃ vācaiti cai duvai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, pādai, pādai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

70.

 tirattānupasampan‍na-sahasaiyyāya uttariṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pan‍nai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

71.

 kappaiti mātugāmaina, sahasaiyyaṃ sacai pana|

dvai sau āpaj‍jatāpattī, rattiyaṃ duk‍kaṭādayau||

72.

 uddhaṃ chappañ‍cavācāhi, dhammaṃ daisaiti itthiyā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, pādai, pādai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

73.

 bhūtaṃ  anupasampan‍nai, manussuttaridhammakaṃ|

āraucaiti sacai tassa, haunti dvai duk‍kaṭādayau||

74.

 vadaṃ anupasampan‍nai, duṭṭhul‍lāpattimañ‍ñatau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, pācittāraucitai siyā||

75.

 pathaviṃ khaṇatau tassa, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ mataṃ|

pahārai ca pahārai ca, pācitti pariyāputā||

musāvādavaggau paṭhamau|

76.

 bhūtagāmaṃ tu pātaintau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, pātai pācitti dīpitā||

77.

 añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ vadantassa, dvai siyuṃ añ‍ñavādakai|

araupitai duk‍kaṭaṃ tu, hauti pācitti raupitai||

78.

 ujjhāpaintau paraṃ bhikkhuṃ, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, ujjhā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

79.

 ajjhaukāsai tu mañ‍cādiṃ, santharitvāna saṅghikaṃ|

pak‍kamantau anāpucchā, āpattiṃ duvidhaṃ phusai||

80.

 laiḍḍupātai  atik‍kantai, pādaina paṭhamaina tu|

duk‍kaṭaṃ, dutiyaināpi, pācitti paridīpayai||

81.

 vihārai saṅghikai saiyyaṃ, santharitvā anuddharaṃ|

anāpucchā pak‍kamantau, duvidhāpattiyau phusai||

82.

 parikkhaipai atik‍kantai, pādaina paṭhamaina tu|

duk‍kaṭaṃ pana uddiṭṭhaṃ, pācitti dutiyaina tu||

83.

 vihārai saṅghikai jānaṃ, pubbūpagatabhikkhukaṃ|

saiyyaṃ kappayatau haunti, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

84.

 saṅghikā kupitau bhikkhuṃ, nik‍kaḍḍhati vihāratau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, saisaṃ nik‍kaḍḍhitai siyā||

85.

 vihārai saṅghikai bhikkhu, vaihāsakuṭiyūpari|

āhac‍capādakai sīdaṃ, phusai dvai duk‍kaṭādayau||

86.

 adhiṭṭhitvā  dvattipariyāyai, uttarimpi adhiṭṭhatau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti panadhiṭṭhitai||

87.

 jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ tauyaṃ, tiṇaṃ vā siñ‍catau pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, sittai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

bhūtagāmavaggau dutiyau|

88.

 phusai bhikkhuniyau bhikkhu, auvadantau asammatau|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tassa, pācittauvaditai siyā||

89.

 dutiyai tatiyai caiva, catutthaipi ca sabbasau|

paṭhamaina samānāva, āpattīnaṃ vibhāgatā||

90.

 cīvaraṃ bhikkhu añ‍ñāti-kāya daintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, din‍nai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

91.

 añ‍ñātikabhikkhuniyā, bhikkhu sibbaiyya cīvaraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, pācitti pana sibbitai||

92.

 addhānañ‍ñatra samayā, bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saha|

saṃvidhāya tu gacchantau, phusai dvai duk‍kaṭādayau||

93.

 nāvaikaṃ abhirūhantau, bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saha|

saṃvidhāya phusai dvaipi, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

94.

 jānaṃ bhikkhuniyā piṇḍa-pātaṃ tu paripācitaṃ|

bhuñ‍jantau duvidhāpatti-māpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau||

95.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti cai bhattaṃ, paṭiggaṇhāti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

ajjhauhārapayaugaisu, pācitti paridīpitā||

96.

 bhikkhu bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ, nisaj‍jaṃ tu rahau pana|

kappaintau hi phusai dvaipi, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

auvādavaggau tatiyau|

97.

 taduttariṃ āvasatha-piṇḍaṃ tu paribhuñ‍jatau|

anantarassa vaggassa, navamaina samau nayau||

98.

 dutiyai  tatiyai cāpi, visaisau natthi kaucipi|

anantarasamānāva, āpattīnaṃ vibhāgatā||

99.

 dvattipattai gahaitvāna, gaṇhatau hi taduttariṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti gahitai siyā||

100.

 pañ‍camai paṭhamainaiva, samau āpattinicchayau|

chaṭṭhai anatirittaina, bhuttāviṃ tu pavāritaṃ||

101.

 abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraintau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

vacanaina ca tassaiva, ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti gaṇhati||

102.

 gahaṇai  duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, piṭakai samudāhaṭaṃ|

bhaujanassa panausānai, pācitti pariyāputā||

103.

 sattamai aṭṭhamai caiva, navamai dasamaipi ca|

paṭhamaina samānāva, āpattīnaṃ vibhāgatā||

bhaujanavaggau catutthau|

104.

 acailakādinau daintau, sahatthā bhaujanādikaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ pattau, din‍nai pācittiyaṃ phusai||

105.

 dāpaitvā vā adāpaitvā, uyyaujaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tasmiṃ, pācittuyyaujitai siyā||

106.

 nisaj‍jaṃ bhikkhu kappaintau, kulai pana sabhaujanai|

āpattiyau phusai dvaipi, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

107.

 catutthai pañ‍camai vāpi, visaisau natthi kaucipi|

tatiyaina samānāva, āpattigaṇanā siyā||

108.

 santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā, sabhattau ca nimantitau|

kulaisu pana cārittaṃ, āpaj‍jantau duvai phusai||

109.

 paṭhamaina ca pādaina, ummārātik‍kamai pana|

duk‍kaṭaṃ piṭakai vuttaṃ, pācitti dutiyaina tu||

110.

 taduttariṃ tu bhaisaj‍jaṃ, viñ‍ñāpaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, viñ‍ñā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

111.

 uyyuttaṃ  dassanatthāya, gacchantau dvai phusai balaṃ|

gacchatau duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, hauti pācitti passatau||

112.

 atiraikatirattaṃ tu, saināya vasatau duvai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti vasitai siyā||

113.

 uyyaudhikaṃ  tu gacchantau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

gacchantau duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, hauti pācitti passatau||

acailakavaggau pañ‍camau|

114.

 suraṃ vā pana mairaiyyaṃ, pivantau dvai phusai muni|

gaṇhatau duk‍kaṭaṃ pātuṃ, pītai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

115.

 bhikkhaṅgulipataudaina, hāsaintau dvai phusai havai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, pācitti hasitai siyā||

116.

 kīḷantau udakai bhikkhu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

duk‍kaṭaṃ gaupphakā haiṭṭhā, pācittuparigaupphakai||

117.

 yau panādariyaṃ bhikkhu, karauntau dvai phusai havai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, katai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

118.

 bhiṃsāpaintau havai bhikkhu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, bhiṃsā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

119.

 jautiṃ samādahitvāna, visibbaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācittiyaṃ visīvitai||

120.

 aurasau addhamāsassa, nhāyantau dvai phusai havai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, nhāna-ssausānai itaraṃ siyā||

121.

 dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ tu, tiṇṇamaikamanādiya|

cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jantau, dvai phusai duk‍kaṭādayau||

122.

 cīvaraṃ bhikkhuādīnaṃ, vikappaitvā anuddharaṃ|

dvai phusai paribhuñ‍jantau, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

123.

 bhikkhussāpanidhaintau  dvai, phusai pattādikaṃ pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tasmiṃ, saisāpanihitai siyā||

surāpānavaggau chaṭṭhau|

124.

 sañ‍cic‍ca  jīvitā pāṇaṃ, vauraupaintau tapaudhanau|

āpattiyau catassauva, āpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau||

125.

 anaudissakamaupātaṃ, khaṇatau hauti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

manussau marati tasmiṃ, patitvā cai parājayau||

126.

 yakkhau vāpi tiracchāna-gatau manussaviggahau|

patitvā maratī paitau, tassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā||

127.

 tiracchānagatai tasmiṃ, nipatitvā matai pana|

tassa pācittiyāpatti, pañ‍ñattā paṭubuddhinā||

128.

 jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ tauyaṃ, paribhuñ‍jaṃ duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, bhuttai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

129.

 nihatādhikaraṇaṃ jānaṃ, uk‍kauṭaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācittuk‍kauṭitai siyā||

130.

 jānaṃ bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍laṃ, chādaintau pana vaj‍jakaṃ|

aikamāpaj‍jatāpattiṃ, pācittimiti dīpitaṃ||

131.

 ūnavīsativassaṃ tu, karauntau upasampadaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ pattau, saisā sampāditai siyā||

132.

 jānaṃ tu thaiyyasatthaina, saṃvidhāya sahaiva ca|

tathaiva mātugāmaina, maggaṃ tu paṭipaj‍jatau||

133.

 dvai panāpattiyau haunti, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ mataṃ|

paṭipan‍nai panuddiṭṭhaṃ, pācittiyamanantaraṃ||

134.

 ac‍cajaṃ  pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

kammavācāya ausānai, hauti pācitti bhikkhunau||

135.

 tathākaṭānudhammaina, saṃbhuñ‍jantau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, bhuttai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

136.

 nāsitaṃ samaṇuddaisaṃpalāpaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti upalāpitai||

sappāṇakavaggau sattamau|

137.

 vuc‍camānassa  bhikkhussa, bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ|

‘‘na sakkhissāmi’’ic‍caivaṃ, bhaṇatau duk‍kaṭādayau||

138.

 vinayaṃ tu vivaṇṇaintau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, pācittaiva vivaṇṇitai||

139.

 mauhaintau dvai phusai, mauhai, duk‍kaṭaṃ tu araupitai|

raupitai pana mauhasmiṃ, pācittiyamudīritaṃ||

140.

 pahāraṃ kupitau daintau, bhikkhussa dvai phusai havai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti pahaṭai siyā||

141.

 bhikkhussa kupitau bhikkhu, uggiraṃ talasattikaṃ|

dvai phusai duk‍kaṭaṃ yaugai, pācittuggiritai siyā||

142.

 bhikkhu saṅghādisaisaina, amūlainaiva caudayaṃ|

dvai phusai duk‍kaṭaṃ yaugai, pācittuddhaṃsitai siyā||

143.

 bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, janayantau hi bhikkhunau|

dvai phusai duk‍kaṭaṃ yaugai, pācittuppāditai siyā||

144.

 tiṭṭhantupassutiṃ bhikkhu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

gacchatau duk‍kaṭaṃ sautuṃ, pācitti suṇatau siyā||

145.

 dhammikānaṃ  tu kammānaṃ, chandaṃ datvā tatau puna|

khīyanadhammamāpaj‍jaṃ, dvai phusai duk‍kaṭādayau||

146.

 saṅghai vinicchayai niṭṭhaṃ, agatai chandamattanau|

adatvā gacchatau tassa, dvai panāpattiyau siyuṃ||

147.

 hatthapāsaṃ tu saṅghassa, jahatau hauti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

jahitai hatthapāsasmiṃ, hauti pācitti bhikkhunau||

148.

 samaggaina ca saṅghaina, datvāna saha cīvaraṃ|

khīyantau dvai phusai pacchā, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

149.

 lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ jānaṃ, saṅghikaṃ puggalassa hi|

dvai phusai pariṇāmaintau, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

sahadhammikavaggau aṭṭhamau|

150.

 pubbai  aviditau hutvā, rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pana|

pavisantassa bhikkhunau, dvai panāpattiyau siyuṃ||

151.

 paṭhamaina ca pādaina, ummārātik‍kamai pana|

duk‍kaṭaṃ pana uddiṭṭhaṃ, pācitti dutiyaina tu||

152.

 ratanaṃ pana gaṇhantau, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa, pācitti gahitai siyā||

153.

 santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā, vikālai gāmakaṃ pana|

samaṇau pavisaṃ dausai, āpaj‍jati duvai pana||

154.

 paṭhamaina ca pādaina, parikkhaipaṃ atik‍kamai|

duk‍kaṭaṃ tassa niddiṭṭhaṃ, pācitti dutiyaina tu||

155.

 aṭṭhidantavisāṇābhi-nibbattaṃ sūciyā gharaṃ|

kārāpaintau phusai dvaipi, payaugai duk‍kaṭādayau||

156.

 pamāṇātītamañ‍cādiṃ, kārāpaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, saisā kārāpitai siyā||

157.

 tūlaunaddhaṃ tu mañ‍cādiṃ, kārāpaintau duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, tasmiṃ, saisā kārāpitai siyā||

158.

 sattamai aṭṭhamai caiva, navamai dasamaipi ca|

anantarasamauyaiva, āpattīnaṃ vinicchayau||

ratanavaggau navamau|

pācittiyakathā|

159.

 catūsu duvidhāpatti, pāṭidaisaniyaisupi|

avisaisaina niddiṭṭhā, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

160.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti bhikkhussa, duk‍kaṭaṃ paṭigaṇhatau|

ajjhauhāraisu sabbattha, pāṭidaisaniyaṃ siyā||

pāṭidaisanīyakathā|

161.

 saikhiyaisu  ca dhammaisu, aikāvāpatti dīpitā|

anādaravasainaiva, duk‍kaṭaṃ samudāhaṭaṃ||

saikhiyakathā|

162.

 pañ‍ñattā maithunaṃ dhammaṃ, paṭisaivanapac‍cayā|

kati āpattiyau haunti? catassauva bhavanti hi||

163.

 maithunaṃ paṭisaivantau, al‍laukāsappavaisanai|

matai akkhāyitai vāpi, bhikkhu pārājikaṃ phusai||

164.

 thul‍lac‍cayaṃ tu yaibhuyya-kkhāyitai, duk‍kaṭaṃ tathā|

mukhai vaṭṭakatai vuttaṃ, pācitti jatumaṭṭhakai||

165.

 pañ‍ñattā  kāyasaṃsaggaṃ, samāpaj‍janapac‍cayā|

kati āpattiyau haunti? pañ‍ca āpattiyau siyuṃ||

166.

 avassutassa pausassa, tathā bhikkhuniyāpi ca|

pārājikamadhakkhādi-gahaṇaṃ sādiyantiyā||

167.

 kāyaina phusatau kāyaṃ, bhikkhussa garukaṃ siyā|

kāyaina kāyabaddhaṃ tu, phusaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā||

168.

 paṭibaddhaina kāyaina, paṭibaddhaṃ tu duk‍kaṭaṃ|

pācittiyaṃ panuddiṭṭhaṃ, tassaṅgulipataudakai||

169.

 saisaisu saikhiyantaisu, āpattīnaṃ vinicchayau|

haiṭṭhā vuttanayainaiva, vaiditabbau vibhāvinā||

mahāvibhaṅgasaṅgahau niṭṭhitau|

bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau

170.

 bhikkhūnaṃ pāṭavatthāya, vinayassa vinicchayai|

bhikkhunīnaṃ vibhaṅgaupi, kiñ‍cimattaṃ bhaṇāmahaṃ||

171.

 avassutassa  pausassa, bhikkhunīpi avassutā|

nandantī kāyasaṃsaggaṃ, kati āpattiyau phusai|

172.

 tissau āpattiyau ubbha-jāṇussādhakkhakassa ca|

hauti pārājikaṃ tassā, gahaṇaṃ sādiyantiyā||

173.

 ubbhakkhakaṃ adhaujāṇu-gahaṇaṃ sādiyantiyā|

thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā, kāya-paṭibaddhai tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

174.

 chādaintī bhikkhunī vaj‍jaṃ, tissau āpattiyau phusai|

jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ, chādaintī sā parājikā||

175.

 thul‍lac‍cayaṃ  vaimatikā, paṭicchādaiti cai pana|

athācāravipattiṃ cai, paṭicchādaiti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

176.

 nissaj‍jantī na taṃ laddhiṃ, ukkhittassānuvattikā|

samanubhāsanāyaiva, tissau āpattiyau phusai||

177.

 ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ, dvīhi, kammavācāhi thul‍latā|

kammavācāya ausānai, pārājikamudīritaṃ||

178.

 pūraintī aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ, tissau āpattiyau phusai|

purisainidhāgacchāti, vuttāgacchati duk‍kaṭaṃ||

179.

 thul‍lac‍cayaṃ tu pausassa, hatthapāsappavaisanai|

pūraintī aṭṭhamaṃ vatthuṃ, samaṇī sā parājitā||

pārājikakathā|

180.

 ussayavādikā aṭṭaṃ, karauntī tividhaṃ phusai|

aikassāraucanai tassā, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

181.

 dutiyāraucanai tassā, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ|

aṭṭassa pariyausānai, hauti saṅghādisaisatā||

182.

 caurivuṭṭhāpikā vāpi, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

dvīhi thul‍lac‍cayaṃ kamma-vācausānai garuṃ siyā||

183.

 aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchai, gamanai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā|

parikkhaipai atik‍kantai, pādaina paṭhamaina tu||

184.

 hauti  thul‍lac‍cayāpatti, tassā samaṇiyā pana|

dutiyaina atik‍kantai, garukai pana tiṭṭhati||

185.

 catutthai dutiyai vutta-sadisauva vinicchayau|

āpattīnaṃ pabhaidai tu, kāci natthi visaisatā||

186.

 avassutā  sayaṃ hutvā, tādisassaiva hatthatau|

gahaitvā pana bhuñ‍jantī, bhaujanādīsu kiñ‍cipi||

187.

 phusai āpattiyau tissau, bhaujanādīsu kiñ‍cipi|

paṭiggaṇhantiyā tassā, hauti thul‍lac‍cayaṃ pana||

188.

 ajjhauhāraisu sabbaisu, hauti saṅghādisaisatā|

udakaṃ dantapaunaṃ vā, paṭiggaṇhāti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

189.

 ‘‘sahatthaina gahaitvā tvaṃ, khāda vā bhuñ‍ja vā’’tipi|

uyyaujaintī panaivaṃ tu, tissau āpattiyau phusai||

190.

 duk‍kaṭaṃ vacanai tassā, ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti gaṇhati|

ajjhauhāraisu sabbaisu, tassā thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā||

191.

 bhaujanassa panausānai, hauti saṅghādisaisatā|

uyyaujaiti ca yā tassā, imā tissauti dīpayai||

192.

 sattamai aṭṭhamai cāpi, navamai dasamaipi ca|

caurivuṭṭhāpanainaiva, samānauva vinicchayau||

saṅghādisaisakathā|

193.

 pattasan‍nicayaṃ yiha, karauntī bhikkhunī pana|

aikaṃ nissaggiyaṃyaiva, phusai pācittiyaṃ tu sā||

194.

 akālacīvaraṃ kāla-cīvaraṃ bhājāpaintiyā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, lābhai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

195.

 cīvaraṃ parivattaitvā, acchindati sacai pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, chin‍nai, tassā nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

196.

 viñ‍ñāpaitvāva añ‍ñaṃ cai, viñ‍ñāpaiti tatau paraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, viñ‍ñā-pitai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

197.

 caitāpaitvā  hi añ‍ñaṃ cai, caitāpaiti tatau paraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, caitā-pitai nissaggiyaṃ siyā||

198.

 aivamaiva ca saisaisu, chaṭṭhādīsu ca sattasu|

anantarasamānauva, āpattīnaṃ vinicchayau||

nissaggiyakathā|

199.

 lasuṇaṃ khādati dvai cai, duk‍kaṭaṃ gahaṇai siyā|

ajjhauhārapayaugaisu, pācitti pariyāputā||

200.

 saṃharāpaintiyā laumaṃ, sambādhai dvaiva haunti hi|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, hauti pācitti saṃhaṭai||

201.

 karauntī talaghātaṃ tu, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, katai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

202.

 jatunā maṭṭhakaṃ kiñ‍ci, sādiyantī duvai phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭādin‍nai, tassā pācittiyaṃ siyā||

203.

 pañ‍camaṃ tu catutthaina, samānamiti dīpayai|

āpattīnaṃ vibhāgasmiṃ, visaisau natthi kaucipi||

204.

 bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jamānassa, pānīyainupatiṭṭhati|

hatthapāsai tu pācitti, hitvā tiṭṭhati duk‍kaṭaṃ||

205.

 viñ‍ñāpaitvāmakaṃ dhañ‍ñaṃ, ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti gaṇhati|

duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti pācitti, ajjhauhāraisu dīpayai||

206.

 uc‍cārādiṃ tiraukuṭṭai, chaḍḍaintī dvai phusai havai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti chaḍḍitai siyā||

207.

 uc‍cārādicatuk‍kaṃ tu, chaḍḍaiti haritai sacai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, pācitti chaḍḍitai siyā||

208.

 nac‍cādiṃ  dassanatthāya, sacai gacchati duk‍kaṭaṃ|

passantiyāpi pācitti, tathaiva ca suṇantiyā||

lasuṇavaggau paṭhamau|

209.

 paṭhamai  dutiyai caiva, tatiyai ca catutthakai|

tulyau lasuṇavaggassa, chaṭṭhainidha vinicchayau||

210.

 kulāni upasaṅkamma, nisīditvā panāsanai|

sāmikai tu anāpucchā, pak‍kamantī duvai phusai||

211.

 paṭhamaina ca pādaina, anauvassamatik‍kamai|

duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti, dutiyātik‍kamai siyā||

212.

 sāmikai tu anāpucchā, āsanai cai nisīdati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti ca nisīditai||

213.

 chaṭṭhaina sattamaṃ sabbaṃ, samānaṃ aṭṭhamai pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, ujjhā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

214.

 attānaṃ cābhisappaintī, dvai phusai nirayādinā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti abhisappitai||

215.

 vadhitvā pana attānaṃ, raudantī tu duvai phusai|

vadhati raudati pācitti, karautaikaṃ tu duk‍kaṭaṃ||

rattandhakāravaggau dutiyau|

216.

 naggā nhāyati dvai caiva, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā|

nhānassa pariyausānai, tassā pācittiyaṃ siyā||

217.

 kārāpaiti  pamāṇāti-k‍kantaṃ udakasāṭikaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, kārā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

218.

 cīvaraṃ tu visibbaitvā, visibbāpaitva vā pana|

naiva sibbantiyā vutta-maikaṃ pācittiyaṃ pana||

219.

 pañ‍cāhikaṃ tu saṅghāṭi-cāraṃ pana atik‍kamai|

aikāvassā panāpatti, pācitti paridīpitā||

220.

 sacai saṅkamanīyaṃ tu, dhāraiti pana cīvaraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti pana dhāritai||

221.

 gaṇacīvaralābhassa, antarāyaṃ karauti cai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, katai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

222.

 vibhaṅgaṃ  paṭibāhantī, cīvarānaṃ tu dhammikaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti paṭibāhitai||

223.

 agārikādinau daiti, sacai samaṇacīvaraṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, din‍nai, pācitti pariyāputā||

224.

 cīvarai dubbalāsāya, kālaṃ cai samatik‍kamai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācitti samatik‍kamai||

225.

 dhammikaṃ kathinuddhāraṃ, paṭibāhantiyā duvai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti paṭibāhitai||

nhānavaggau tatiyau|

226.

 duvai bhikkhuniyau aika-mañ‍casmiṃ cai tuvaṭṭaiyyuṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tāsaṃ, nipan‍nai itaraṃ siyā||

227.

 dutiyaṃ paṭhamainaiva, sadisaṃ tatiyai pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, katai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

228.

 nupaṭṭhāpaintiyā  vāpi, dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ|

aikāyaiva panāpatti, pācitti paridīpitā||

229.

 sacai upassayaṃ datvā, nik‍kaḍḍhati ca bhikkhuniṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, hauti pācitti kaḍḍhitai||

230.

 chaṭṭhai pana ca saṃsaṭṭhā, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

kammavācāya ausānai, pācitti paridīpitā||

231.

 antauraṭṭhai tu sāsaṅkai, cārikaṃ tu carantiyā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, paṭipan‍nāya saisakaṃ||

232.

 aṭṭhamaṃ navamañ‍caiva, sattamaina samaṃ mataṃ|

dasamai pana aikāva, pācitti paridīpitā||

tuvaṭṭavaggau catutthau|

233.

 rājāgārādikaṃ sabbaṃ, dassanatthāya gacchati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, pācitti yadi passati||

234.

 āsandiṃ  vāpi pal‍laṅkaṃ, paribhuñ‍jantiyā duvai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, bhuttai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

235.

 suttaṃ kantantiyā dvaiva, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ mataṃ|

uj‍javuj‍javanai tassā, pācitti samudāharai||

236.

 vaiyyāvac‍caṃ gihīnaṃ tu, dvaiva haunti karauntiyā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, katai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

237.

 pañ‍camai pana aikāva, pācitti paridīpitā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ chaṭṭhai, din‍nai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

238.

 sattamaṃ dutiyainaiva, samāpattipabhaidatau|

aṭṭhamaṃ dutiyai vaggai, pañ‍camaina samaṃ mataṃ||

239.

 tiracchānagataṃ  vij‍jaṃ, dvaiva haunti paṭhantiyā|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti hi padai padai||

240.

 dasamaṃ navamainaiva, samānaṃ sabbathā pana|

‘‘pariyāpuṇāti, vācaiti’’, padamattaṃ visaisakaṃ||

cittāgāravaggau pañ‍camau|

241.

 sabhikkhukaṃ tamārāmaṃ, jānantī pana bhikkhunī|

pavisantī anāpucchā, dvai panāpattiyau phusai||

242.

 paṭhamaina ca pādaina, parikkhaipassatik‍kamai|

duk‍kaṭaṃ piṭakai vuttaṃ, pācitti dutiyaina tu||

243.

 ak‍kausati ca yā bhikkhuṃ, bhikkhunī paribhāsati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, pācittak‍kausitai siyā||

244.

 yā hi caṇḍikabhāvaina, gaṇaṃ tu paribhāsati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, paribhaṭṭhai panaitaraṃ||

245.

 nimantitā pavāritā, khādanaṃ bhaujanampi vā|

bhuñ‍jantī bhikkhunī sā hi, dvai panāpattiyau phusai||

246.

 ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti yaṃ kiñ‍ci, paṭiggaṇhāti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

ajjhauhārapayaugaisu, pācitti paridīpayai||

247.

 kulaṃ  tu maccharāyantī, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, saisā maccharitai siyā||

248.

 abhikkhukai panāvāsai, bhavai vassaṃ vasantiyā|

duk‍kaṭaṃ pubbakic‍caisu, pācitti aruṇuggamai||

249.

 bhikkhunī ubhatausaṅghai, vassaṃvuṭṭhā tu tīhipi|

ṭhānaihi appavāraintī, aikaṃ pācittiyaṃ phusai||

250.

 auvādatthāya  vā bhikkhuṃ, saṃvāsatthāya vā tathā|

na gacchati sacai tassā, aikaṃ pācittiyaṃ siyā||

251.

 auvādampi na yācantī, na gacchantī upausathaṃ|

aikaṃ pācittiyāpatti-māpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau||

252.

 apucchitvāva saṅghaṃ vā, bhaidāpaiti pasākhajaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, bhin‍nai, pācitti pariyāputā||

ārāmavaggau chaṭṭhau|

253.

 gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhapaintī hi, dvai panāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, vuṭṭhā-pitai pācittiyaṃ siyā||

254.

 dutiyaṃ tatiyañ‍caiva, catutthaṃ pañ‍camampi ca|

chaṭṭhañ‍ca sattamañ‍caiva, paṭhamaina samaṃ mataṃ||

255.

 bhikkhunī vuṭṭhapaitvāna, bhikkhuniṃ sahajīviniṃ|

dvaivassaṃ nānuggaṇhantī, aikaṃ pācittiyaṃ phusai||

256.

 navamaṃ dasamañ‍caiva, aṭṭhamaina samaṃ mataṃ|

dvīsu āpattibhaidasmiṃ, nānattaṃ natthi kiñ‍cipi||

gabbhinīvaggau sattamau|

257.

 kumārībhūtavaggassa, āditau pana pañ‍capi|

samānā gabbhinīvaggai, paṭhamainaiva sabbasau||

258.

 ‘‘alaṃ vuṭṭhāpitainā’’ti, vuc‍camānā hi khīyati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, pacchā, hauti pācitti khīyitai||

259.

 sattamai  aṭṭhamai caiva, aikaṃ pācittiyaṃ mataṃ|

ādināva samānāni, navamādīni pañ‍capi||

kumārībhūtavaggau aṭṭhamau|

260.

 āpattiyau phusai dvaipi, dhāraintī chattupāhanaṃ|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, hauti pācitti dhāritai||

261.

 yānaina pana yāyantī, dvai kirāpattiyau phusai|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, pācitti yadi yāyitai||

262.

 dhāraintiyā tu saṅghāṇiṃ, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ siyā|

dhāritai pana pācitti, catutthaipi ayaṃ nayau||

263.

 nhāyantī gandhavaṇṇaina, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

nhānassa pariyausānai, tassā pācittiyaṃ siyā||

264.

 chaṭṭhampi pañ‍camainaiva, samānaṃ sabbathā pana|

sattamai aṭṭhamai caiva, navamai dasamaipi ca||

265.

 payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, pācittummadditai siyā|

āpattīnaṃ vibhāgasmiṃ, natthi kāci visaisatā||

266.

 anāpucchā tu bhikkhussa, puratau yā nisīdati|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ tassā, pācitti tu nisīditai||

267.

 anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ, pañhaṃ pucchantiyā pana|

payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ hauti, vuttā pācitti pucchitai||

268.

 saṃkac‍cikaṃ vinā gāmaṃ, padasā pavisantiyā|

paṭhamainaiva ārāma-vaggassa sadisaṃ vadai||

chattupāhanavaggau navamau|

pācittiyakathā|

269.

 aṭṭhasu  duvidhāpatti, pāṭidaisaniyaisupi|

viñ‍ñāpaitvā sacai sappiṃ, ‘‘bhuñ‍jissāmī’’ti gaṇhati||

270.

 tatau  bhikkhuniyā tassā, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ|

ajjhauhāraisu sabbaisu, pāṭidaisaniyaṃ siyā||

pāṭidaisanīyakathā|

271.

 imaṃ viditvā paramaṃ panuttaraṃ|

niruttaraṃ atthavasaina bhikkhu|

sukhaina pañ‍ñattamahāsamuddaṃ|

duruttaraṃ uttarataiva dhīrau||

272.

 yasmā tasmā asmiṃ yaugaṃ|

usmāyuttau yuttau kātuṃ|

sattau sattau kaṅkhacchaidai|

satthai satthai nic‍caṃ nic‍caṃ||

bhikkhunīvibhaṅgau niṭṭhitau|

catuvipattikathā

273.

 kati āpattiyau sīla-vipattipac‍cayā pana|

catassauva siyuṃ sīla-vipattipac‍cayā pana||

274.

 jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ, sacai chādaiti bhikkhunī|

cutā, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ hauti, sacai vaimatikā siyā||

275.

 pācitti bhikkhu saṅghādi-saisaṃ chādaiti cai pana|

attanau pana duṭṭhul‍laṃ, chādaintau duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai||

276.

 āpattiyau  katācāra-vipattipac‍cayā pana|

aikāyaiva siyācāra-vipattipac‍cayā pana||

277.

 paṭicchādaiti ācāra-vipattiṃ pana bhikkhu cai|

aikamaivassa bhikkhussa, hauti āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

278.

 kati āpattiyau diṭṭhi-vipattipac‍cayā pana?

dvai panāpattiyau diṭṭhi-vipattipac‍cayā siyuṃ||

279.

 ac‍cajaṃ  pāpikaṃ diṭṭhiṃ, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

kammavācāya ausānai, pācitti pariyāputā||

280.

 āpattiyau katājīva-vipattipac‍cayā pana?

chaḷaivāpaj‍jatājīva-vipattipac‍cayā pana||

281.

 ājīvahaitu pāpicchau, asantaṃ pana attani|

manussuttaridhammaṃ tu, vadaṃ pārājikaṃ phusai||

282.

 sañ‍carittaṃ samāpan‍nau, hauti saṅghādisaisatā|

pariyāyavacanai ñātai, tassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ siyā||

283.

 paṇītabhaujanaṃ vatvā, pācitti paribhuñ‍jatau|

bhikkhunī tu sacai hauti, pāṭidaisaniyaṃ siyā||

284.

 ājīvahaitu sūpaṃ vā, audanaṃ vā panattanau|

atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvāna, duk‍kaṭaṃ paribhuñ‍jatau||

catuvipattikathā|

adhikaraṇapac‍cayakathā

285.

 vivādādhikaraṇamhā, kati āpattiyau siyuṃ?

vivādādhikaraṇamhā, dvai panāpattiyau siyuṃ||

286.

 pācitti  upasampan‍naṃ, hauti aumasatau pana|

bhikkhussānupasampan‍naṃ, aumasantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ||

287.

 anuvādādhikaraṇa-pac‍cayāpattiyau kati?

anuvādādhikaraṇa-pac‍cayā tividhā siyuṃ||

288.

 anuddhaṃsaiti cai bhikkhuṃ, amūlantimavatthunā|

saṅghādisaisamāpatti-māpaj‍jati, na saṃsayau||

289.

 tathā saṅghādisaisaina, anuddhaṃsaiti cai pana|

pācitti, duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, tathācāravipattiyā||

290.

 āpattipac‍cayā  vuttā, kati āpattiyau pana?

āpattipac‍cayā vuttā, catassauva mahaisinā||

291.

 jānaṃ pārājikaṃ dhammaṃ, sacai chādaiti bhikkhunī|

cutā, thul‍lac‍cayaṃ hauti, sacai vaimatikā siyā||

292.

 pācitti bhikkhu saṅghādi-saisaṃ chādaiti cai pana|

tathācāravipattiṃ tu, sacai chādaiti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

293.

 āpattiyau hi kic‍cādhi-karaṇapac‍cayā kati?

pañ‍caiva haunti kic‍cādhi-karaṇapac‍cayā pana||

294.

 samanubhāsanāyaiva, ñattiyā duk‍kaṭaṃ phusai|

samaṇī ac‍cajantīva, ukkhittassānuvattikā||

295.

 thul‍lac‍cayaṃ dvayaṃ dvīhi, kammavācāhi sā phusai|

kammavācāya ausānai, tassā pārājikaṃ siyā||

296.

 samanubhāsanāyaiva, bhaidakassānuvattikā|

na paric‍cajati taṃ laddhiṃ, hauti saṅghādisaisatā||

297.

 samanubhāsanāyaiva, pāpikāya ca diṭṭhiyā|

yāvatatiyakaṃ tassā, pācittac‍cajataupi ca||

adhikaraṇapac‍cayakathā|

khandhakapucchākathā

298.

 khandhakaisupi  āpatti-pabhaidaṃ āgataṃ pana|

pāṭavatthāya bhikkhūnaṃ, pavakkhāmi nibaudhatha||

299.

 khandhakai paṭhamai tāva, kati āpattiyau siyuṃ?

khandhakai paṭhamai tāva, dvai panāpattiyau siyuṃ||

300.

 ūnavīsativassaṃ tu, karautau upasampadaṃ|

hauti pācittiyaṃ tassa, saisaisu pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

301.

 kati  āpattiyau haunti|

khandhakai tu upausathai?

tissau āpattiyau haunti|

khandhakai tu upausathai||

302.

 ‘‘nassantaitai vinassantu’’, iti bhaidapurakkhakā|

upausathassa karaṇai, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ||

303.

 ukkhittakaina saddhiṃ tu, karauntassa upausathaṃ|

hauti pācittiyaṃ tassa, saisaisu pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

304.

 kati āpattiyau vuttā, vada vassūpanāyikai?

aikāva duk‍kaṭāpatti, vuttā vassūpanāyikai||

305.

 kati āpattiyau vuttā, khandhakai tu pavāraṇai?

tissau āpattiyau vuttā, upausathasamā matā||

306.

 kati āpattiyau vuttā, cammai? tissauva dīpitā|

māraintānaṃ tu pācitti, gahaitvā vacchatariṃ pana||

307.

 aṅgajātaṃ chupantassa, rattaina pana caitasā|

tassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ vuttaṃ, saisaisu pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

308.

 kati āpattiyau vuttā, bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakai pana?

tissau āpattiyau vuttā, bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakai pana||

309.

 samantā  dvaṅgulai tattha, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ|

bhauj‍jayāgūsu pācitti, saisaisu pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

310.

 kathinai natthi āpatti, pañ‍ñattaṃ kaivalaṃ pana|

kati cīvarasaṃyuttai, vuttā āpattiyau pana?

311.

 tissau cīvarasaṃyuttai, vuttā āpattiyau pana|

kusavākamayai cīrai, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ||

312.

 sanissaggāva pācitti, atiraikai tu cīvarai|

saisaisu duk‍kaṭaṃ vuttaṃ, buddhainādic‍cabandhunā||

313.

 campaiyyakai ca kausambai, kammasmiṃ pārivāsikai|

tathā samuc‍cayai aikā, duk‍kaṭāpatti dīpitā||

314.

 kati  āpattiyau vuttā, samathakkhandhakai pana?

dvaiyaivāpattiyau vuttā, samathakkhandhakai pana||

315.

 chandassa dāyakau bhikkhu, pācitti yadi khīyati|

saisaisu pana sabbattha, duk‍kaṭaṃ samudāhaṭaṃ||

316.

 kati khuddakavatthusmiṃ, vuttā āpattiyau pana?

tissau khuddakavatthusmiṃ, vuttā āpattiyau pana||

317.

 attanau aṅgajātaṃ tu, chindaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ phusai|

raumanthai hauti pācitti, saisai āpatti duk‍kaṭaṃ||

318.

 tathā saināsanasmiṃ tu, tissau āpattiyau siyuṃ|

vissaj‍janai ca garunau, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ||

319.

 nik‍kaḍḍhanai ca pācitti, saṅghikamhā vihāratau|

saisaisu pana sabbattha, duk‍kaṭaṃ samudāhaṭaṃ||

320.

 kati āpattiyau saṅgha-bhaidai vuttā mahaisinā?

dvai panāpattiyau saṅgha-bhaidai vuttā mahaisinā||

321.

 bhaidānuvattakānaṃ  tu, thul‍lac‍cayamudīritaṃ|

gaṇabhaugai tu bhikkhūnaṃ, pācitti paridīpitā||

322.

 khandhakai vattasaṃyuttai, kati āpattiyau matā?

khandhakai vattasaṃyuttai, duk‍kaṭāpattiyaiva sā||

323.

 ṭhapanai pātimaukkhassa, tathā aikāva dīpitā|

bhikkhunikkhandhakai cāpi, kati āpattiyau matā?

324.

 bhikkhunikkhandhakai cāpi, dvai panāpattiyau matā|

apavāraṇāya pācitti, saisaisu pana duk‍kaṭaṃ||

khandhakapucchākathā niṭṭhitā|

samuṭṭhānasīsakathā

325.

 vibhaṅgaisu  pana dvīsu, pañ‍ñattāni mahaisinā|

yāni pārājikādīni, uddisanti upausathai||

326.

 taisaṃ dāni pavakkhāmi, samuṭṭhānamitau paraṃ|

pāṭavatthāya bhikkhūnaṃ, taṃ suṇātha samāhitā||

327.

 kāyau ca vācāpi ca kāyavācā|

tānaiva cittaina yutāni tīṇi|

aikaṅgikaṃ dvaṅgitivaṅgikanti|

chadhā samuṭṭhānavidhiṃ vadanti||

328.

 taisu aikaina vā dvīhi, tīhi vātha catūhi vā|

chahi vāpattiyau nānā-samuṭṭhānaihi jāyarai||

329.

 tattha pañ‍casamuṭṭhānā, kā cāpatti na vij‍jati|

hauti aikasamuṭṭhānā, pacchimaihaiva tīhipi||

330.

 tathaiva  dvisamuṭṭhānā, kāyatau kāyacittatau|

vācatau vācacittamhā, tatiyacchaṭṭhataupi ca||

331.

 catutthacchaṭṭhatau caiva, pañ‍camacchaṭṭhataupi ca|

jāyatai pañ‍cadhāvaisā, samuṭṭhāti na añ‍ñatau||

332.

 tisamuṭṭhānikā nāma, paṭhamaihi ca tīhipi|

pacchimaihi ca tīhaiva, samuṭṭhāti na añ‍ñatau||

333.

 paṭhamā tatiyā caiva, catutthacchaṭṭhataupi ca|

dutiyā tatiyā caiva, pañ‍camacchaṭṭhataupi ca||

334.

 dvidhā catusamuṭṭhānā, jāyatai na panañ‍ñatau|

aikadhā chasamuṭṭhānā, samuṭṭhāti chahaiva hi||

āha ca –

335.

 ‘‘tidhā aikasamuṭṭhānā, pañ‍cadhā dvisamuṭṭhitā|

dvidhā ticaturau ṭhānā, aikadhā chasamuṭṭhitā’’||

336.

 tairasaiva  ca nāmāni, samuṭṭhānavisaisatau|

labhantāpattiyau sabbā, tāni vakkhāmitau paraṃ||

337.

 paṭhamantimavatthuñ‍ca, dutiyaṃ sañ‍carittakaṃ|

samanubhāsanañ‍caiva, kathinaiḷakalaumakaṃ||

338.

 padasaudhammamaddhānaṃ, thaiyyasatthañ‍ca daisanā|

bhūtāraucanakañ‍caiva, caurivuṭṭhāpanampi ca||

339.

 ananuñ‍ñātakañ‍cāti, sīsānaitāni tairasa|

tairasaitai samuṭṭhāna-nayā viñ‍ñūhi cintitā||

340.

 tattha yā tu catutthaina, samuṭṭhānaina jāyatai|

ādipārājikuṭṭhānā, ayanti paridīpitā||

341.

 sacittakaihi  tīhaiva, samuṭṭhānaihi yā pana|

jāyatai sā panuddiṭṭhā, adin‍nādānapubbakā||

342.

 samuṭṭhānaihi yāpatti, jātucchahipi jāyatai|

sañ‍carittasamuṭṭhānā, nāmāti paridīpitā||

343.

 chaṭṭhainaiva samuṭṭhāti, samuṭṭhānaina yā pana|

samuṭṭhānavasaināyaṃ, vuttā samanubhāsanā||

344.

 tatiyacchaṭṭhatauyaiva, samuṭṭhāti hi yā pana|

samuṭṭhānavasaināyaṃ, kathinupapadā matā||

345.

 jāyatai yā panāpatti, kāyatau kāyacittatau|

ayamaiḷakalaumādi-samuṭṭhānāti dīpitā||

346.

 jāyatai yā panāpatti, vācatau vācacittatau|

ayaṃ tu padasaudhamma-samuṭṭhānāti vuc‍cati||

347.

 kāyatau kāyavācamhā, catutthacchaṭṭhataupi ca|

jāyatai sā panaddhāna-samuṭṭhānāti sūcitā||

348.

 catutthacchaṭṭhatauyaiva, samuṭṭhāti hi yā pana|

thaiyyasatthasamuṭṭhānā, ayanti paridīpitā||

349.

 pañ‍camainaiva yā caittha, samuṭṭhānaina jāyatai|

samuṭṭhānavasaināyaṃ, dhammadaisanasañ‍ñitā||

350.

 acittakaihi  tīhaiva, samuṭṭhānaihi yā siyā|

samuṭṭhānavasaināyaṃ, bhūtāraucanapubbakā||

351.

 pañ‍camacchaṭṭhatauyaiva, yā samuṭṭhānatau siyā|

ayaṃ tu paṭhitā cauri-vuṭṭhāpanasamuṭṭhitā||

 dutiyā tatiyamhā ca, pañ‍camacchaṭṭhataupi yā|
